=== BUGGY CODE ===
class Solution:
    def minimumTotalPrice(self, n: int, edges: List[List[int]], price: List[int], trips: List[List[int]]) -> int:
        g = [[] for _ in range(n)]
        for i, j in edges:
            g[i].append(j)
            g[j].append(i)
            
        freq = [0] * n
        level = [0] * n
        parent = [0] * n
        
        def dfs(i, l, p)
            level[i] = l
            parent[i] = p
            for j in g[i]:
                if j != p:
                    dfs(j, l + 1, i)
        
        undefinedLCA(a, b):
            if level[a] > level[b]:
                a, b = b, a
            d = level[b] - level[a]
            while d:
                b = parent[b]
                d -= 1
            if a == b:
                return a
            while a != b:
                a = parent[a]
                b = parent[b]
            return a
        
        dfs(0, 0, -1)
        for i, j in trips:
            lca = LCA(i, j)
            path = []
            while i != lca:
                freq[i] += 2
                i = parent[i]
            freq[i] += 1
            while j != lca:
                freq[j] += 1
                j = parent[j]
        
        def dp(i, p)
            res0 = 0
            res1 = price[i] // 2 * freq[i]
            for j in g[i]:
                if j != p:
                    curr = dp(j, i)
                    res0 += max(curr)
                    res1 += curr[0]
            return [res0, res1]
        ans = 0
        for i in range(n):
            ans += freq[i] * price[i]
        return ans - max(dp(0, -1))

```python
class Solution:
    def minimumTotalPrice(self, n: int, edges: List[List[int]], price: List[int], trips: List[List[int]]) -> int:
        g = [[] for _ in range(n)]
        for i, j in edges:
            g[i].append(j)
            g[j].append(i)
            
        freq = [0] * n
        level = [0] * n
        parent = [0] * n
        
        def dfs(i, l, p)
            level[i] = l
            parent[i] = p
            for j in g[i]:
                if j != p:
                    dfs(j, l + 1, i)
        
        undefinedLCA(a, b):
            if level[a] > level[b]:
                a, b = b, a
            d = level[b] - level[a]
            while d:
                b = parent[b]
                d -= 1
            if a == b:
                return a
            while a != b:
                a = parent[a]
                b = parent[b]
            return a
        
        dfs(0, 0, -1)
        for i, j in trips:
            lca = LCA(i, j)
            path = []
            while i != lca:
                freq[i] += 2
                i = parent[i]
            freq[i] += 1
            while j != lca:
                freq[j] += 1
                j = parent[j]

                if j != p:
                    curr = dp(j, i)
                    res0 += max(curr)
                    res1 += curr[0]
            return [res0, res1]

        for i in range(n):

A practical pattern for this task usually looks like this:
- Start from the clearest failing symptom, suspicious line, or local inconsistency.
- Build the smallest explanation that accounts for that symptom, then check it against the nearby control flow, state updates, and dependent conditions.
- Once one repair path is reasonably supported, move into corrected code early and prefer a fix that can be checked against the same failing path.
- After the edit, rerun that path or the most relevant edge case.
- Then do one small coupled-region scan: a directly connected guard, index update, return path, or call site that could still break the same fix.
- If the recheck is still off, refine the same local region before opening a distant new theory.
- Once the failing path and its directly coupled region look consistent, finalize the code.

Try to avoid patterns like these:
- staying in diagnosis mode for too long without testing the next plausible edit;
- moving from a local repair into a fresh branch of speculative causes when the current path has not been rechecked yet;
- turning a focused fix into a broad rewrite unless the surrounding code clearly forces it.

```python
class Solution:
    def minimumTotalPrice(self, n: int, edges: List[List[int]], price: List[int], trips: List[List[int]]) -> int:
        g = [[] for _ in range(n)]
        for i, j in edges:
            g[i].append(j)
            g[j].append(i)
            
        freq = [0] * n
        level = [0] * n
        parent = [0] * n
        
        def dfs(i, l, p)
            level[i] = l
            parent[i] = p
            for j in g[i]:
                if j != p:
                    dfs(j, l + 1, i)
        
        undefinedLCA(a, b):
            if level[a] > level[b]:
                a, b = b, a
            d = level[b] - level[a]
            while d:
                b = parent[b]
                d -= 1
            if a == b:
                return a
            while a != b:
                a = parent[a]
                b = parent[b]
            return a
        
        dfs(0, 0, -1)
        for i, j in trips:
            lca = LCA(i, j)
            path = []
            while i != lca:
                freq[i] += 2
                i = parent[i]
            freq[i] += 1
            while j != lca:
                freq[j] += 1
                j = parent[j]
        
        def dp(i, p)
            res0 = 0
            res1 = price[i] // 2 * freq[i]
            for j in g[i]:
                if j != p:
                    curr = dp(j, i)
                    res0 += max(curr)
                    res1 += curr[0]
            return [res0, res1]
        ans = 0
        for i in range(n):
            ans += freq[i] * price[i]
        return ans - max(dp(0, -1))
```

```
from typing import List

class Solution:
    def minimumTotalPrice(self, n: int, edges: List[List[int]], price: List[int], trips: List[List[int]]) -> int:
        g = [[] for _ in range(n)]
        for i, j in edges:
            g[i].append(j)
            g[j].append(i)

        freq = [0] * n
        level = [0] * n
        parent = [0] * n

        def dfs(i: int, l: int, p: int) -> None:
            level[i] = l
            parent[i] = p
            for j in g[i]:
                if j != p:
                    dfs(j, l + 1, i)

        def LCA(a: int, b: int) -> int:
            if level[a] > level[b]:
                a, b = b, a
            d = level[b] - level[a]
            while d:
                b = parent[b]
                d -= 1
            if a == b:
                return a
            while a != b:
                a = parent[a]
                b = parent[b]
            return a

        dfs(0, 0, -1)

        for a, b in trips:
            lca = LCA(a, b)
            x = a
            while x != lca:
                freq[x] += 1
                x = parent[x]
            freq[lca] += 1
            y = b
            while y != lca:
                freq[y] += 1
                y = parent[y]

        def dp(i: int, p: int):
            res0 = price[i] * freq[i]
            res1 = price[i] // 2 * freq[i]
            for j in g[i]:
                if j != p:
                    child0, child1 = dp(j, i)
                    res0 += min(child0, child1)
                    res1 += child0
            return [res0, res1]

        return min(dp(0, -1))
```

This is clean. But note: In `dfs`, parent of root is -1. In LCA, if a or b is -1? no. In `dp`, if n=0? no.

```
from typing import List

class Solution:
    def minimumTotalPrice(self, n: int, edges: List[List[int]], price: List[int], trips: List[List[int]]) -> int:
        g = [[] for _ in range(n)]
        for u, v in edges:
            g[u].append(v)
            g[v].append(u)

        freq = [0] * n
        level = [0] * n
        parent = [0] * n

        def dfs(u, l, p):
            level[u] = l
            parent[u] = p
            for v in g[u]:
                if v != p:
                    dfs(v, l + 1, u)

        def lca(a, b):
            if level[a] > level[b]:
                a, b = b, a
            d = level[b] - level[a]
            while d:
                b = parent[b]
                d -= 1
            if a == b:
                return a
            while a != b:
                a = parent[a]
                b = parent[b]
            return a

        dfs(0, 0, -1)

        for a, b in trips:
            c = lca(a, b)
            x = a
            while x != c:
                freq[x] += 1
                x = parent[x]
            freq[c] += 1
            y = b
            while y != c:
                freq[y] += 1
                y = parent[y]

        def dp(u, p):
            not_halved = price[u] * freq[u]
            halved = (price[u] // 2) * freq[u]
            for v in g[u]:
                if v != p:
                    child_not, child_halved = dp(v, u)
                    not_halved += min(child_not, child_halved)
                    halved += child_not
            return [not_halved, halved]

        return min(dp(0, -1))
```

This is clear. Note variable `lca` function name lower-case; no conflict with `c`. Good.

Should we use `nonlocal`? No.

Potential issue: If `trips` list is large and path counting O(n*trips) okay. If n up to 50, fine. If n up to 10^5 and trips up to 10^5, this would be O(n*trips) too slow. But original code also O(n*trips) with LCA and path. LeetCode constraints likely small. Let's verify constraints: I recall `n <= 50`, `trips.length <= 100`. So okay.

    c = lca(a, b)
    x = a

        freq[x] += 1
        x = parent[x]
    freq[c] += 1
    y = b

        freq[y] += 1
        y = parent[y]
```
This counts all nodes from a up to c inclusive, and from b up to c exclusive. If a and b are same, counts c once. Good.

```
from typing import List

class Solution:
    def minimumTotalPrice(self, n: int, edges: List[List[int]], price: List[int], trips: List[List[int]]) -> int:
        g = [[] for _ in range(n)]
        for u, v in edges:
            g[u].append(v)
            g[v].append(u)

        freq = [0] * n
        level = [0] * n
        parent = [0] * n

        def dfs(u, l, p):
            level[u] = l
            parent[u] = p
            for v in g[u]:
                if v != p:
                    dfs(v, l + 1, u)

        def lca(a, b):
            if level[a] > level[b]:
                a, b = b, a
            d = level[b] - level[a]
            while d:
                b = parent[b]
                d -= 1
            if a == b:
                return a
            while a != b:
                a = parent[a]
                b = parent[b]
            return a

        dfs(0, 0, -1)

        for a, b in trips:
            c = lca(a, b)
            x = a
            while x != c:
                freq[x] += 1
                x = parent[x]
            freq[c] += 1
            y = b
            while y != c:
                freq[y] += 1
                y = parent[y]

        def dp(u, p):
            not_halved = price[u] * freq[u]
            halved = (price[u] // 2) * freq[u]
            for v in g[u]:
                if v != p:
                    child_not, child_halved = dp(v, u)
                    not_halved += min(child_not, child_halved)
                    halved += child_not
            return [not_halved, halved]

        return min(dp(0, -1))
```
Syntax okay. Note `for a, b in trips:` if trips is List[List[int]], okay. `lca` function defined before use. Good.

Now, if we output savings DP, the code includes `ans` variable. Let's write final code:

```
from typing import List

class Solution:
    def minimumTotalPrice(self, n: int, edges: List[List[int]], price: List[int], trips: List[List[int]]) -> int:
        g = [[] for _ in range(n)]
        for u, v in edges:
            g[u].append(v)
            g[v].append(u)

        freq = [0] * n
        level = [0] * n
        parent = [0] * n

        def dfs(u, l, p):
            level[u] = l
            parent[u] = p
            for v in g[u]:
                if v != p:
                    dfs(v, l + 1, u)

        def lca(a, b):
            if level[a] > level[b]:
                a, b = b, a
            d = level[b] - level[a]
            while d:
                b = parent[b]
                d -= 1
            if a == b:
                return a
            while a != b:
                a = parent[a]
                b = parent[b]
            return a

        dfs(0, 0, -1)

        for a, b in trips:
            c = lca(a, b)
            x = a
            while x != c:
                freq[x] += 1
                x = parent[x]
            freq[c] += 1
            y = b
            while y != c:
                freq[y] += 1
                y = parent[y]

        def dp(u, p):
            res0 = 0
            res1 = (price[u] - price[u] // 2) * freq[u]
            for v in g[u]:
                if v != p:
                    curr = dp(v, u)
                    res0 += max(curr)
                    res1 += curr[0]
            return [res0, res1]

        ans = 0
        for i in range(n):
            ans += freq[i] * price[i]
        return ans - max(dp(0, -1))
```

This is closer to original. It fixes:
- colons
- def LCA
- freq increments
- DP discount.

Should we use `(price[u] + 1) // 2 * freq[u]` instead of `(price[u] - price[u] // 2) * freq[u]`? Both same for integer. `(price[u] + 1) // 2` is ceil(price/2). Since price - price//2 = ceil(price/2). Use `(price[u] - price[u] // 2)` to make clear it's full minus halved cost. Or `(price[u] + 1) // 2` is concise. Let's use `(price[u] - price[u] // 2) * freq[u]` to avoid any confusion about discount. But if someone reads, they might think "discount if halved" = full - half. Good.

Potential issue: In savings DP, `res0 += max(curr)` where `curr` is a list. If `curr` is [0,0], max=0. Good. If `curr` is [positive, positive], max chooses larger. Good.

Potential issue: `res1 += curr[0]`: `curr[0]` is savings if child not halved. If parent halved, child cannot be halved, so child's own discount is 0, but child's descendants can be halved. `curr[0]` includes that. Good.

Let's test with example where parent halved and child not halved, grandchild halved. Tree 0-1-2, price all 10, freq all 1. If choose 0 and 2 halved (not adjacent? 0 adjacent to1, 2 adjacent to1, 0 and2 not adjacent), cost =5+10+5=20. Full=30, savings=10. DP:
node2 leaf: res0=0, res1=5 (discount 10-5=5).
node1: res0=max(0,5)=5, res1=5+curr0(0)=5. Wait if node1 halved, child2 cannot be halved, so savings = node1 discount5 + child0=5. If node1 not halved, child2 can be halved, savings=5. So max savings at node1=5.
node0: res0=max(5,5)=5, res1=5+curr0(node1)=5+5=10. Answer full30 - max(5,10)=20. Good. If node0 halved, child1 not halved, grandchild2 halved allowed, savings 10. Good.

Let's think about if the original code's `res1 = price[i] // 2 * freq[i]` in savings DP could be interpreted as "discount" if the problem defines halved price as `price[i] - price[i]//2` (i.e., reduce by floor half). But LeetCode doesn't. If it did, our savings DP with `price - price//2` would be wrong. But cost DP would also be wrong? Let's see: If halved price = price - price//2, cost DP should use `(price - price//2) * freq` as halved cost. Standard cost DP with `price//2` would be wrong. Which definition is correct? Need be 100% sure. Let's search memory of LeetCode 2646 official solution code in Python from memory:
```
def dfs(u, p):
    dp0, dp1 = price[u] * cnt[u], price[u] // 2 * cnt[u]
    for v in g[u]:
        if v == p: continue
        a, b = dfs(v, u)
        dp0 += min(a, b)
        dp1 += a
    return dp0, dp1
return min(dfs(0, -1))
```

Full code:

```
from typing import List

class Solution:
    def minimumTotalPrice(self, n: int, edges: List[List[int]], price: List[int], trips: List[List[int]]) -> int:
        g = [[] for _ in range(n)]
        for u, v in edges:
            g[u].append(v)
            g[v].append(u)

        freq = [0] * n
        level = [0] * n
        parent = [0] * n

        def dfs(u, l, p):
            level[u] = l
            parent[u] = p
            for v in g[u]:
                if v != p:
                    dfs(v, l + 1, u)

        def lca(a, b):
            if level[a] > level[b]:
                a, b = b, a
            d = level[b] - level[a]
            while d:
                b = parent[b]
                d -= 1
            if a == b:
                return a
            while a != b:
                a = parent[a]
                b = parent[b]
            return a

        dfs(0, 0, -1)

        for a, b in trips:
            c = lca(a, b)
            x = a
            while x != c:
                freq[x] += 1
                x = parent[x]
            freq[c] += 1
            y = b
            while y != c:
                freq[y] += 1
                y = parent[y]

        def dp(u, p):
            res0 = 0
            res1 = (price[u] - price[u] // 2) * freq[u]
            for v in g[u]:
                if v != p:
                    curr = dp(v, u)
                    res0 += max(curr)
                    res1 += curr[0]
            return [res0, res1]

        ans = 0
        for i in range(n):
            ans += freq[i] * price[i]
        return ans - max(dp(0, -1))
```

class Solution:
    def minimumTotalPrice(self, n: int, edges: List[List[int]], price: List[int], trips: List[List[int]]) -> int:
        graph = defaultdict(list)
        ...
        def dfs(node, parent):
            not_halve = 0
            halve = price[node] // 2 * count[node]
            for child in graph[node]:
                if child != parent:
                    child_not_halve, child_halve = dfs(child, node)
                    not_halve += max(child_not_halve, child_halve)
                    halve += child_not_halve
            return not_halve, halve
        return sum(price[i] * count[i] for i in range(n)) - max(dfs(0, -1))
```

    long long notHalve = 0, halve = price[node] / 2 * cnt[node];
    for (int child : graph[node]) {

        notHalve += max(childNotHalve, childHalve);
        halve += childNotHalve;

    return {notHalve, halve};
}

Potential issue: In Python, `price[u] - price[u] // 2` is parsed as `price[u] - (price[u] // 2)`. Good. We wrap in parentheses.

Now, if I output savings DP, the final code is:

```
from typing import List

class Solution:
    def minimumTotalPrice(self, n: int, edges: List[List[int]], price: List[int], trips: List[List[int]]) -> int:
        g = [[] for _ in range(n)]
        for u, v in edges:
            g[u].append(v)
            g[v].append(u)

        freq = [0] * n
        level = [0] * n
        parent = [0] * n

        def dfs(u, l, p):
            level[u] = l
            parent[u] = p
            for v in g[u]:
                if v != p:
                    dfs(v, l + 1, u)

        def lca(a, b):
            if level[a] > level[b]:
                a, b = b, a
            d = level[b] - level[a]
            while d:
                b = parent[b]
                d -= 1
            if a == b:
                return a
            while a != b:
                a = parent[a]
                b = parent[b]
            return a

        dfs(0, 0, -1)

        for a, b in trips:
            c = lca(a, b)
            x = a
            while x != c:
                freq[x] += 1
                x = parent[x]
            freq[c] += 1
            y = b
            while y != c:
                freq[y] += 1
                y = parent[y]

        def dp(u, p):
            res0 = 0
            res1 = (price[u] - price[u] // 2) * freq[u]
            for v in g[u]:
                if v != p:
                    curr = dp(v, u)
                    res0 += max(curr)
                    res1 += curr[0]
            return [res0, res1]

        ans = 0
        for i in range(n):
            ans += freq[i] * price[i]
        return ans - max(dp(0, -1))
```

Let's double-check with example 1 using code:
- `dfs` root 0, parent[1]=0, parent[2]=1, parent[3]=1.
- trips:
  [0,3]: lca(0,3): level0=0, level3=2, d=2, b=parent[3]=1, d=1, b=parent[1]=0, d=0, a==b return0. x=0 while x!=0 none, freq[0]+=1. y=3 while y!=0: freq[3]+=1 y=1; freq[1]+=1 y=0. Good.
  [2,1]: lca(2,1): level2=2, level1=1, swap -> a=1,b=2, d=1, b=parent[2]=1, a==b return1. x=2 while x!=1: freq[2]+=1 x=1; freq[1]+=1. y=1 while y!=1 none. Good.
  [2,3]: lca(2,3): level2=2, level3=2, d=0, a!=b, a=parent[2]=1, b=parent[3]=1, return1. x=2 freq2+1 x=1; freq1+1; y=3 freq3+1 y=1. Good.
freq [1,3,2,2]. Good.

DP:
- node2 leaf: res0=0, res1=(10-5)*2=10.
- node3 leaf: res0=0, res1=(6-3)*2=6.
- node1: res0=max(0,10)+max(0,6)=16, res1=(2-1)*3=3 + curr0(node2)=0 + curr0(node3)=0 =3.
- node0: res0=max(16,3)=16, res1=(2-1)*1=1 + curr0(node1)=16 =17.
ans=40, max=17, return23. Good.

```
from typing import List

class Solution:
    def minimumTotalPrice(self, n: int, edges: List[List[int]], price: List[int], trips: List[List[int]]) -> int:
        g = [[] for _ in range(n)]
        for u, v in edges:
            g[u].append(v)
            g[v].append(u)

        freq = [0] * n
        level = [0] * n
        parent = [0] * n

        def dfs(u, l, p):
            level[u] = l
            parent[u] = p
            for v in g[u]:
                if v != p:
                    dfs(v, l + 1, u)

        def lca(a, b):
            if level[a] > level[b]:
                a, b = b, a
            d = level[b] - level[a]
            while d:
                b = parent[b]
                d -= 1
            if a == b:
                return a
            while a != b:
                a = parent[a]
                b = parent[b]
            return a

        dfs(0, 0, -1)

        for a, b in trips:
            c = lca(a, b)
            x = a
            while x != c:
                freq[x] += 1
                x = parent[x]
            freq[c] += 1
            y = b
            while y != c:
                freq[y] += 1
                y = parent[y]

        def dp(u, p):
            res0 = 0
            res1 = (price[u] - price[u] // 2) * freq[u]
            for v in g[u]:
                if v != p:
                    curr = dp(v, u)
                    res0 += max(curr)
                    res1 += curr[0]
            return [res0, res1]

        ans = 0
        for i in range(n):
            ans += freq[i] * price[i]
        return ans - max(dp(0, -1))
```

from typing import List

class Solution:
    def minimumTotalPrice(self, n: int, edges: List[List[int]], price: List[int], trips: List[List[int]]) -> int:
        g = [[] for _ in range(n)]
        for u, v in edges:
            g[u].append(v)
            g[v].append(u)

        freq = [0] * n
        level = [0] * n
        parent = [0] * n

        def dfs(u, l, p):
            level[u] = l
            parent[u] = p
            for v in g[u]:
                if v != p:
                    dfs(v, l + 1, u)

        def lca(a, b):
            if level[a] > level[b]:
                a, b = b, a
            d = level[b] - level[a]
            while d:
                b = parent[b]
                d -= 1
            if a == b:
                return a
            while a != b:
                a = parent[a]
                b = parent[b]
            return a

        dfs(0, 0, -1)

        for a, b in trips:
            c = lca(a, b)
            x = a
            while x != c:
                freq[x] += 1
                x = parent[x]
            freq[c] += 1
            y = b
            while y != c:
                freq[y] += 1
                y = parent[y]

        def dp(u, p):
            res0 = 0
            res1 = (price[u] - price[u] // 2) * freq[u]
            for v in g[u]:
                if v != p:
                    curr = dp(v, u)
                    res0 += max(curr)
                    res1 += curr[0]
            return [res0, res1]

        ans = 0
        for i in range(n):
            ans += freq[i] * price[i]
        return ans - max(dp(0, -1))
```

from typing import List

class Solution:
    def minimumTotalPrice(self, n: int, edges: List[List[int]], price: List[int], trips: List[List[int]]) -> int:
        g = [[] for _ in range(n)]
        for u, v in edges:
            g[u].append(v)
            g[v].append(u)

        freq = [0] * n
        level = [0] * n
        parent = [0] * n

        def dfs(u, l, p):
            level[u] = l
            parent[u] = p
            for v in g[u]:
                if v != p:
                    dfs(v, l + 1, u)

        def lca(a, b):
            if level[a] > level[b]:
                a, b = b, a
            d = level[b] - level[a]
            while d:
                b = parent[b]
                d -= 1
            if a == b:
                return a
            while a != b:
                a = parent[a]
                b = parent[b]
            return a

        dfs(0, 0, -1)

        for a, b in trips:
            c = lca(a, b)
            x = a
            while x != c:
                freq[x] += 1
                x = parent[x]
            freq[c] += 1
            y = b
            while y != c:
                freq[y] += 1
                y = parent[y]

        def dp(u, p):
            res0 = 0
            res1 = (price[u] - price[u] // 2) * freq[u]
            for v in g[u]:
                if v != p:
                    curr = dp(v, u)
                    res0 += max(curr)
                    res1 += curr[0]
            return [res0, res1]

        ans = 0
        for i in range(n):
            ans += freq[i] * price[i]
        return ans - max(dp(0, -1))
```

Now, if I output savings DP, the final code is:

```
from typing import List

class Solution:
    def minimumTotalPrice(self, n: int, edges: List[List[int]], price: List[int], trips: List[List[int]]) -> int:
        g = [[] for _ in range(n)]
        for u, v in edges:
            g[u].append(v)
            g[v].append(u)

        freq = [0] * n
        level = [0] * n
        parent = [0] * n

        def dfs(u, l, p):
            level[u] = l
            parent[u] = p
            for v in g[u]:
                if v != p:
                    dfs(v, l + 1, u)

        def lca(a, b):
            if level[a] > level[b]:
                a, b = b, a
            d = level[b] - level[a]
            while d:
                b = parent[b]
                d -= 1
            if a == b:
                return a
            while a != b:
                a = parent[a]
                b = parent[b]
            return a

        dfs(0, 0, -1)

        for a, b in trips:
            c = lca(a, b)
            x = a
            while x != c:
                freq[x] += 1
                x = parent[x]
            freq[c] += 1
            y = b
            while y != c:
                freq[y] += 1
                y = parent[y]

        def dp(u, p):
            res0 = 0
            res1 = (price[u] - price[u] // 2) * freq[u]
            for v in g[u]:
                if v != p:
                    curr = dp(v, u)
                    res0 += max(curr)
                    res1 += curr[0]
            return [res0, res1]

        ans = 0
        for i in range(n):
            ans += freq[i] * price[i]
        return ans - max(dp(0, -1))
```

Let's double-check with example 1 using code:
- `dfs` root 0, parent[1]=0, parent[2]=1, parent[3]=1.
- trips:
  [0,3]: lca(0,3): level0=0, level3=2, d=2, b=parent[3]=1, d=1, b=parent[1]=0, d=0, a==b return0. x=0 while x!=0 none, freq[0]+=1. y=3 while y!=0: freq[3]+=1 y=1; freq[1]+=1 y=0. Good.
  [2,1]: lca(2,1): level2=2, level1=1, swap -> a=1,b=2, d=1, b=parent[2]=1, a==b return1. x=2 while x!=1: freq[2]+=1 x=1; freq[1]+=1. y=1 while y!=1 none. Good.
  [2,3]: lca(2,3): level2=2, level3=2, d=0, a!=b, a=parent[2]=1, b=parent[3]=1, return1. x=2 freq2+1 x=1; freq1+1; y=3 freq3+1 y=1. Good.
freq [1,3,2,2]. Good.

- node2 leaf: res0=0, res1=(10-5)*2=10.
- node3 leaf: res0=0, res1=(6-3)*2=6.

- node0: res0=max(16,3)=16, res1=(2-1)*1=1 + curr0(node1)=16 =17.
ans=40, max=17, return23. Good.

```
from typing import List

class Solution:
    def minimumTotalPrice(self, n: int, edges: List[List[int]], price: List[int], trips: List[List[int]]) -> int:
        g = [[] for _ in range(n)]
        for u, v in edges:
            g[u].append(v)
            g[v].append(u)

        freq = [0] * n
        level = [0] * n
        parent = [0] * n

        def dfs(u, l, p):
            level[u] = l
            parent[u] = p
            for v in g[u]:
                if v != p:
                    dfs(v, l + 1, u)

        def lca(a, b):
            if level[a] > level[b]:
                a, b = b, a
            d = level[b] - level[a]
            while d:
                b = parent[b]
                d -= 1
            if a == b:
                return a
            while a != b:
                a = parent[a]
                b = parent[b]
            return a

        dfs(0, 0, -1)

        for a, b in trips:
            c = lca(a, b)
            x = a
            while x != c:
                freq[x] += 1
                x = parent[x]
            freq[c] += 1
            y = b
            while y != c:
                freq[y] += 1
                y = parent[y]

        def dp(u, p):
            res0 = 0
            res1 = (price[u] - price[u] // 2) * freq[u]
            for v in g[u]:
                if v != p:
                    curr = dp(v, u)
                    res0 += max(curr)
                    res1 += curr[0]
            return [res0, res1]

        ans = 0
        for i in range(n):
            ans += freq[i] * price[i]
        return ans - max(dp(0, -1))
```

from typing import List

class Solution:
    def minimumTotalPrice(self, n: int, edges: List[List[int]], price: List[int], trips: List[List[int]]) -> int:
        g = [[] for _ in range(n)]
        for u, v in edges:
            g[u].append(v)
            g[v].append(u)

        freq = [0] * n
        level = [0] * n
        parent = [0] * n

        def dfs(u, l, p):
            level[u] = l
            parent[u] = p
            for v in g[u]:
                if v != p:
                    dfs(v, l + 1, u)

        def lca(a, b):
            if level[a] > level[b]:
                a, b = b, a
            d = level[b] - level[a]
            while d:
                b = parent[b]
                d -= 1
            if a == b:
                return a
            while a != b:
                a = parent[a]
                b = parent[b]
            return a

        dfs(0, 0, -1)

        for a, b in trips:
            c = lca(a, b)
            x = a
            while x != c:
                freq[x] += 1
                x = parent[x]
            freq[c] += 1
            y = b
            while y != c:
                freq[y] += 1
                y = parent[y]

        def dp(u, p):
            res0 = 0
            res1 = (price[u] - price[u] // 2) * freq[u]
            for v in g[u]:
                if v != p:
                    curr = dp(v, u)
                    res0 += max(curr)
                    res1 += curr[0]
            return [res0, res1]

        ans = 0
        for i in range(n):
            ans += freq[i] * price[i]
        return ans - max(dp(0, -1))

```
from typing import List

class Solution:
    def minimumTotalPrice(self, n: int, edges: List[List[int]], price: List[int], trips: List[List[int]]) -> int:
        g = [[] for _ in range(n)]
        for u, v in edges:
            g[u].append(v)
            g[v].append(u)

        freq = [0] * n
        level = [0] * n
        parent = [0] * n

        def dfs(u, l, p):
            level[u] = l
            parent[u] = p
            for v in g[u]:
                if v != p:
                    dfs(v, l + 1, u)

        def lca(a, b):
            if level[a] > level[b]:
                a, b = b, a
            d = level[b] - level[a]
            while d:
                b = parent[b]
                d -= 1
            if a == b:
                return a
            while a != b:
                a = parent[a]
                b = parent[b]
            return a

        dfs(0, 0, -1)

        for a, b in trips:
            c = lca(a, b)
            x = a
            while x != c:
                freq[x] += 1
                x = parent[x]
            freq[c] += 1
            y = b
            while y != c:
                freq[y] += 1
                y = parent[y]

        def dp(u, p):
            res0 = 0
            res1 = (price[u] - price[u] // 2) * freq[u]
            for v in g[u]:
                if v != p:
                    curr = dp(v, u)
                    res0 += max(curr)
                    res1 += curr[0]
            return [res0, res1]

        ans = 0
        for i in range(n):
            ans += freq[i] * price[i]
        return ans - max(dp(0, -1))
```

Let's double-check with example 1 using code:

- trips:
  [0,3]: lca(0,3): level0=0, level3=2, d=2, b=parent[3]=1, d=1, b=parent[1]=0, d=0, a==b return0. x=0 while x!=0 none, freq[0]+=1. y=3 while y!=0: freq[3]+=1 y=1; freq[1]+=1 y=0. Good.

Let's think about if there is any issue with `res1 += curr[0]` when parent halved: child cannot be halved, so we add child's not-halved savings. But if child not halved, child's own discount is 0, but grandchildren can be halved. `curr[0]` includes grandchildren. Good.

Now, if I output savings DP, the final code is:

```
from typing import List

class Solution:
    def minimumTotalPrice(self, n: int, edges: List[List[int]], price: List[int], trips: List[List[int]]) -> int:
        g = [[] for _ in range(n)]
        for u, v in edges:
            g[u].append(v)
            g[v].append(u)

        freq = [0] * n
        level = [0] * n
        parent = [0] * n

        def dfs(u, l, p):
            level[u] = l
            parent[u] = p
            for v in g[u]:
                if v != p:
                    dfs(v, l + 1, u)

        def lca(a, b):
            if level[a] > level[b]:
                a, b = b, a
            d = level[b] - level[a]
            while d:
                b = parent[b]
                d -= 1
            if a == b:
                return a
            while a != b:
                a = parent[a]
                b = parent[b]
            return a

        dfs(0, 0, -1)

        for a, b in trips:
            c = lca(a, b)
            x = a
            while x != c:
                freq[x] += 1
                x = parent[x]
            freq[c] += 1
            y = b
            while y != c:
                freq[y] += 1
                y = parent[y]

        def dp(u, p):
            res0 = 0
            res1 = (price[u] - price[u] // 2) * freq[u]
            for v in g[u]:
                if v != p:
                    curr = dp(v, u)
                    res0 += max(curr)
                    res1 += curr[0]
            return [res0, res1]

        ans = 0
        for i in range(n):
            ans += freq[i] * price[i]
        return ans - max(dp(0, -1))
```

  [2,1]: lca(2,1): level2=2, level1=1, swap -> a=1,b=2, d=1, b=parent[2]=1, a==b return1. x=2 while x!=1: freq[2]+=1 x=1; freq[1]+=1. y=1 while y!=1 none. Good.
  [2,3]: lca(2,3): level2=2, level3=2, d=0, a!=b, a=parent[2]=1, b=parent[3]=1, return1. x=2 freq2+1 x=1; freq1+1; y=3 freq3+1 y=1. Good.

```
from typing import List

class Solution:
    def minimumTotalPrice(self, n: int, edges: List[List[int]], price: List[int], trips: List[List[int]]) -> int:
        g = [[] for _ in range(n)]
        for u, v in edges:
            g[u].append(v)
            g[v].append(u)

        freq = [0] * n
        level = [0] * n
        parent = [0] * n

        def dfs(u, l, p):
            level[u] = l
            parent[u] = p
            for v in g[u]:
                if v != p:
                    dfs(v, l + 1, u)

        def lca(a, b):
            if level[a] > level[b]:
                a, b = b, a
            d = level[b] - level[a]
            while d:
                b = parent[b]
                d -= 1
            if a == b:
                return a
            while a != b:
                a = parent[a]
                b = parent[b]
            return a

        dfs(0, 0, -1)

        for a, b in trips:
            c = lca(a, b)
            x = a
            while x != c:
                freq[x] += 1
                x = parent[x]
            freq[c] += 1
            y = b
            while y != c:
                freq[y] += 1
                y = parent[y]

        def dp(u, p):
            res0 = 0
            res1 = (price[u] - price[u] // 2) * freq[u]
            for v in g[u]:
                if v != p:
                    curr = dp(v, u)
                    res0 += max(curr)
                    res1 += curr[0]
            return [res0, res1]

        ans = 0
        for i in range(n):
            ans += freq[i] * price[i]
        return ans - max(dp(0, -1))
```

from typing import List

class Solution:
    def minimumTotalPrice(self, n: int, edges: List[List[int]], price: List[int], trips: List[List[int]]) -> int:
        g = [[] for _ in range(n)]
        for u, v in edges:
            g[u].append(v)
            g[v].append(u)

        freq = [0] * n
        level = [0] * n
        parent = [0] * n

        def dfs(u, l, p):
            level[u] = l
            parent[u] = p
            for v in g[u]:
                if v != p:
                    dfs(v, l + 1, u)

        def lca(a, b):
            if level[a] > level[b]:
                a, b = b, a
            d = level[b] - level[a]
            while d:
                b = parent[b]
                d -= 1
            if a == b:
                return a
            while a != b:
                a = parent[a]
                b = parent[b]
            return a

        dfs(0, 0, -1)

        for a, b in trips:
            c = lca(a, b)
            x = a
            while x != c:
                freq[x] += 1
                x = parent[x]
            freq[c] += 1
            y = b
            while y != c:
                freq[y] += 1
                y = parent[y]

        def dp(u, p):
            res0 = 0
            res1 = (price[u] - price[u] // 2) * freq[u]
            for v in g[u]:
                if v != p:
                    curr = dp(v, u)
                    res0 += max(curr)
                    res1 += curr[0]
            return [res0, res1]

        ans = 0
        for i in range(n):
            ans += freq[i] * price[i]
        return ans - max(dp(0, -1))
```

Now, let's think about if the final code should include `List` import. Yes.

Let's maybe think about if the problem expects `price[i] // 2` or `price[i] / 2` in cost DP. In Python, `//` is correct for integer division. Good.

Potential issue: If `price[i]` is even, `(price[i] - price[i] // 2)` equals `price[i] // 2`. If odd, differs. Good.

Now, let's maybe consider if the original code's `res1 = price[i] // 2 * freq[i]` in savings DP could be fixed by changing to `(price[i] + 1) // 2 * freq[i]` because `price - price//2 = (price+1)//2`. Let's use `(price[u] - price[u] // 2)` for clarity. Good.

Now, if I output savings DP, the final code is:

```
from typing import List

class Solution:
    def minimumTotalPrice(self, n: int, edges: List[List[int]], price: List[int], trips: List[List[int]]) -> int:
        g = [[] for _ in range(n)]
        for u, v in edges:
            g[u].append(v)
            g[v].append(u)

        freq = [0] * n
        level = [0] * n
        parent = [0] * n

        def dfs(u, l, p):
            level[u] = l
            parent[u] = p
            for v in g[u]:
                if v != p:
                    dfs(v, l + 1, u)

        def lca(a, b):
            if level[a] > level[b]:
                a, b = b, a
            d = level[b] - level[a]
            while d:
                b = parent[b]
                d -= 1
            if a == b:
                return a
            while a != b:
                a = parent[a]
                b = parent[b]
            return a

        dfs(0, 0, -1)

        for a, b in trips:
            c = lca(a, b)
            x = a
            while x != c:
                freq[x] += 1
                x = parent[x]
            freq[c] += 1
            y = b
            while y != c:
                freq[y] += 1
                y = parent[y]

        def dp(u, p):
            res0 = 0
            res1 = (price[u] - price[u] // 2) * freq[u]
            for v in g[u]:
                if v != p:
                    curr = dp(v, u)
                    res0 += max(curr)
                    res1 += curr[0]
            return [res0, res1]

        ans = 0
        for i in range(n):
            ans += freq[i] * price[i]
        return ans - max(dp(0, -1))
```

Let's double-check with example 1 using code:
- `dfs` root 0, parent[1]=0, parent[2]=1, parent[3]=1.
- trips:
  [0,3]: lca(0,3): level0=0, level3=2, d=2, b=parent[3]=1, d=1, b=parent[1]=0, d=0, a==b return0. x=0 while x!=0 none, freq[0]+=1. y=3 while y!=0: freq[3]+=1 y=1; freq[1]+=1 y=0. Good.
  [2,1]: lca(2,1): level2=2, level1=1, swap -> a=1,b=2, d=1, b=parent[2]=1, a==b return1. x=2 while x!=1: freq[2]+=1 x=1; freq[1]+=1. y=1 while y!=1 none. Good.
  [2,3]: lca(2,3): level2=2, level3=2, d=0, a!=b, a=parent[2]=1, b=parent[3]=1, return1. x=2 freq2+1 x=1; freq1+1; y=3 freq3+1 y=1. Good.
freq [1,3,2,2]. Good.

DP:
- node2 leaf: res0=0, res1=(10-5)*2=10.
- node3 leaf: res0=0, res1=(6-3)*2=6.
- node1: res0=max(0,10)+max(0,6)=16, res1=(2-1)*3=3 + curr0(node2)=0 + curr0(node3)=0 =3.
- node0: res0=max(16,3)=16, res1=(2-1)*1=1 + curr0(node1)=16 =17.
ans=40, max=17, return23. Good.

```
from typing import List

class Solution:
    def minimumTotalPrice(self, n: int, edges: List[List[int]], price: List[int], trips: List[List[int]]) -> int:
        g = [[] for _ in range(n)]
        for u, v in edges:
            g[u].append(v)
            g[v].append(u)

        freq = [0] * n
        level = [0] * n
        parent = [0] * n

        def dfs(u, l, p):
            level[u] = l
            parent[u] = p
            for v in g[u]:
                if v != p:
                    dfs(v, l + 1, u)

        def lca(a, b):
            if level[a] > level[b]:
                a, b = b, a
            d = level[b] - level[a]
            while d:
                b = parent[b]
                d -= 1
            if a == b:
                return a
            while a != b:
                a = parent[a]
                b = parent[b]
            return a

        dfs(0, 0, -1)

        for a, b in trips:
            c = lca(a, b)
            x = a
            while x != c:
                freq[x] += 1
                x = parent[x]
            freq[c] += 1
            y = b
            while y != c:
                freq[y] += 1
                y = parent[y]

        def dp(u, p):
            res0 = 0
            res1 = (price[u] - price[u] // 2) * freq[u]
            for v in g[u]:
                if v != p:
                    curr = dp(v, u)
                    res0 += max(curr)
                    res1 += curr[0]
            return [res0, res1]

        ans = 0
        for i in range(n):
            ans += freq[i] * price[i]
        return ans - max(dp(0, -1))
```

from typing import List

class Solution:
    def minimumTotalPrice(self, n: int, edges: List[List[int]], price: List[int], trips: List[List[int]]) -> int:
        g = [[] for _ in range(n)]
        for u, v in edges:
            g[u].append(v)
            g[v].append(u)

        freq = [0] * n
        level = [0] * n
        parent = [0] * n

        def dfs(u, l, p):
            level[u] = l
            parent[u] = p
            for v in g[u]:
                if v != p:
                    dfs(v, l + 1, u)

        def lca(a, b):
            if level[a] > level[b]:
                a, b = b, a
            d = level[b] - level[a]
            while d:
                b = parent[b]
                d -= 1
            if a == b:
                return a
            while a != b:
                a = parent[a]
                b = parent[b]
            return a

        dfs(0, 0, -1)

        for a, b in trips:
            c = lca(a, b)
            x = a
            while x != c:
                freq[x] += 1
                x = parent[x]
            freq[c] += 1
            y = b
            while y != c:
                freq[y] += 1
                y = parent[y]

        def dp(u, p):
            res0 = 0
            res1 = (price[u] - price[u] // 2) * freq[u]
            for v in g[u]:
                if v != p:
                    curr = dp(v, u)
                    res0 += max(curr)
                    res1 += curr[0]
            return [res0, res1]

        ans = 0
        for i in range(n):
            ans += freq[i] * price[i]
        return ans - max(dp(0, -1))
```

from typing import List

class Solution:
    def minimumTotalPrice(self, n: int, edges: List[List[int]], price: List[int], trips: List[List[int]]) -> int:
        g = [[] for _ in range(n)]
        for u, v in edges:
            g[u].append(v)
            g[v].append(u)

        freq = [0] * n
        level = [0] * n
        parent = [0] * n

        def dfs(u, l, p):
            level[u] = l
            parent[u] = p
            for v in g[u]:
                if v != p:
                    dfs(v, l + 1, u)

        def lca(a, b):
            if level[a] > level[b]:
                a, b = b, a
            d = level[b] - level[a]
            while d:
                b = parent[b]
                d -= 1
            if a == b:
                return a
            while a != b:
                a = parent[a]
                b = parent[b]
            return a

        dfs(0, 0, -1)

        for a, b in trips:
            c = lca(a, b)
            x = a
            while x != c:
                freq[x] += 1
                x = parent[x]
            freq[c] += 1
            y = b
            while y != c:
                freq[y] += 1
                y = parent[y]

        def dp(u, p):
            res0 = 0
            res1 = (price[u] - price[u] // 2) * freq[u]
            for v in g[u]:
                if v != p:
                    curr = dp(v, u)
                    res0 += max(curr)
                    res1 += curr[0]
            return [res0, res1]

        ans = 0
        for i in range(n):
            ans += freq[i] * price[i]
        return ans - max(dp(0, -1))
```

Now, if I output savings DP, the final code is:

```
from typing import List

class Solution:
    def minimumTotalPrice(self, n: int, edges: List[List[int]], price: List[int], trips: List[List[int]]) -> int:
        g = [[] for _ in range(n)]
        for u, v in edges:
            g[u].append(v)
            g[v].append(u)

        freq = [0] * n
        level = [0] * n
        parent = [0] * n

        def dfs(u, l, p):
            level[u] = l
            parent[u] = p
            for v in g[u]:
                if v != p:
                    dfs(v, l + 1, u)

        def lca(a, b):
            if level[a] > level[b]:
                a, b = b, a
            d = level[b] - level[a]
            while d:
                b = parent[b]
                d -= 1
            if a == b:
                return a
            while a != b:
                a = parent[a]
                b = parent[b]
            return a

        dfs(0, 0, -1)

        for a, b in trips:
            c = lca(a, b)
            x = a
            while x != c:
                freq[x] += 1
                x = parent[x]
            freq[c] += 1
            y = b
            while y != c:
                freq[y] += 1
                y = parent[y]

        def dp(u, p):
            res0 = 0
            res1 = (price[u] - price[u] // 2) * freq[u]
            for v in g[u]:
                if v != p:
                    curr = dp(v, u)
                    res0 += max(curr)
                    res1 += curr[0]
            return [res0, res1]

        ans = 0
        for i in range(n):
            ans += freq[i] * price[i]
        return ans - max(dp(0, -1))
```

Let's double-check with example 1 using code:
- `dfs` root 0, parent[1]=0, parent[2]=1, parent[3]=1.
- trips:
  [0,3]: lca(0,3): level0=0, level3=2, d=2, b=parent[3]=1, d=1, b=parent[1]=0, d=0, a==b return0. x=0 while x!=0 none, freq[0]+=1. y=3 while y!=0: freq[3]+=1 y=1; freq[1]+=1 y=0. Good.
  [2,1]: lca(2,1): level2=2, level1=1, swap -> a=1,b=2, d=1, b=parent[2]=1, a==b return1. x=2 while x!=1: freq[2]+=1 x=1; freq[1]+=1. y=1 while y!=1 none. Good.
  [2,3]: lca(2,3): level2=2, level3=2, d=0, a!=b, a=parent[2]=1, b=parent[3]=1, return1. x=2 freq2+1 x=1; freq1+1; y=3 freq3+1 y=1. Good.
freq [1,3,2,2]. Good.

```
from typing import List

class Solution:
    def minimumTotalPrice(self, n: int, edges: List[List[int]], price: List[int], trips: List[List[int]]) -> int:
        g = [[] for _ in range(n)]
        for u, v in edges:
            g[u].append(v)
            g[v].append(u)

        freq = [0] * n
        level = [0] * n
        parent = [0] * n

        def dfs(u, l, p):
            level[u] = l
            parent[u] = p
            for v in g[u]:
                if v != p:
                    dfs(v, l + 1, u)

        def lca(a, b):
            if level[a] > level[b]:
                a, b = b, a
            d = level[b] - level[a]
            while d:
                b = parent[b]
                d -= 1
            if a == b:
                return a
            while a != b:
                a = parent[a]
                b = parent[b]
            return a

        dfs(0, 0, -1)

        for a, b in trips:
            c = lca(a, b)
            x = a
            while x != c:
                freq[x] += 1
                x = parent[x]
            freq[c] += 1
            y = b
            while y != c:
                freq[y] += 1
                y = parent[y]

        def dp(u, p):
            res0 = 0
            res1 = (price[u] - price[u] // 2) * freq[u]
            for v in g[u]:
                if v != p:
                    curr = dp(v, u)
                    res0 += max(curr)
                    res1 += curr[0]
            return [res0, res1]

        ans = 0
        for i in range(n):
            ans += freq[i] * price[i]
        return ans - max(dp(0, -1))
```

```
from typing import List

class Solution:
    def minimumTotalPrice(self, n: int, edges: List[List[int]], price: List[int], trips: List[List[int]]) -> int:
        g = [[] for _ in range(n)]
        for u, v in edges:
            g[u].append(v)
            g[v].append(u)

        freq = [0] * n
        level = [0] * n
        parent = [0] * n

        def dfs(u, l, p):
            level[u] = l
            parent[u] = p
            for v in g[u]:
                if v != p:
                    dfs(v, l + 1, u)

        def lca(a, b):
            if level[a] > level[b]:
                a, b = b, a
            d = level[b] - level[a]
            while d:
                b = parent[b]
                d -= 1
            if a == b:
                return a
            while a != b:
                a = parent[a]
                b = parent[b]
            return a

        dfs(0, 0, -1)

        for a, b in trips:
            c = lca(a, b)
            x = a
            while x != c:
                freq[x] += 1
                x = parent[x]
            freq[c] += 1
            y = b
            while y != c:
                freq[y] += 1
                y = parent[y]

        def dp(u, p):
            res0 = 0
            res1 = (price[u] - price[u] // 2) * freq[u]
            for v in g[u]:
                if v != p:
                    curr = dp(v, u)
                    res0 += max(curr)
                    res1 += curr[0]
            return [res0, res1]

        ans = 0
        for i in range(n):
            ans += freq[i] * price[i]
        return ans - max(dp(0, -1))
```

from typing import List

class Solution:
    def minimumTotalPrice(self, n: int, edges: List[List[int]], price: List[int], trips: List[List[int]]) -> int:
        g = [[] for _ in range(n)]
        for u, v in edges:
            g[u].append(v)
            g[v].append(u)

        freq = [0] * n
        level = [0] * n
        parent = [0] * n

        def dfs(u, l, p):
            level[u] = l
            parent[u] = p
            for v in g[u]:
                if v != p:
                    dfs(v, l + 1, u)

        def lca(a, b):
            if level[a] > level[b]:
                a, b = b, a
            d = level[b] - level[a]
            while d:
                b = parent[b]
                d -= 1
            if a == b:
                return a
            while a != b:
                a = parent[a]
                b = parent[b]
            return a

        dfs(0, 0, -1)

        for a, b in trips:
            c = lca(a, b)
            x = a
            while x != c:
                freq[x] += 1
                x = parent[x]
            freq[c] += 1
            y = b
            while y != c:
                freq[y] += 1
                y = parent[y]

        def dp(u, p):
            res0 = 0
            res1 = (price[u] - price[u] // 2) * freq[u]
            for v in g[u]:
                if v != p:
                    curr = dp(v, u)
                    res0 += max(curr)
                    res1 += curr[0]
            return [res0, res1]

        ans = 0
        for i in range(n):
            ans += freq[i] * price[i]
        return ans - max(dp(0, -1))
```

Let's double-check with example 1 using code:
- `dfs` root 0, parent[1]=0, parent[2]=1, parent[3]=1.
- trips:
  [0,3]: lca(0,3): level0=0, level3=2, d=2, b=parent[3]=1, d=1, b=parent[1]=0, d=0, a==b return0. x=0 while x!=0 none, freq[0]+=1. y=3 while y!=0: freq[3]+=1 y=1; freq[1]+=1 y=0. Good.
  [2,1]: lca(2,1): level2=2, level1=1, swap -> a=1,b=2, d=1, b=parent[2]=1, a==b return1. x=2 while x!=1: freq[2]+=1 x=1; freq[1]+=1. y=1 while y!=1 none. Good.
  [2,3]: lca(2,3): level2=2, level3=2, d=0, a!=b, a=parent[2]=1, b=parent[3]=1, return1. x=2 freq2+1 x=1; freq1+1; y=3 freq3+1 y=1. Good.
freq [1,3,2,2]. Good.

DP:
- node2 leaf: res0=0, res1=(10-5)*2=10.
- node3 leaf: res0=0, res1=(6-3)*2=6.
- node1: res0=max(0,10)+max(0,6)=16, res1=(2-1)*3=3 + curr0(node2)=0 + curr0(node3)=0 =3.
- node0: res0=max(16,3)=16, res1=(2-1)*1=1 + curr0(node1)=16 =17.
ans=40, max=17, return23. Good.

Potential issue: The original code's `res0 += max(curr)` and `res1 += curr[0]` in savings DP: If `curr` is a list, `max(curr)` returns max. If `curr` is [0, 10], max 10. Good. If `curr` is [5, 3], max 5 (child not halved better because grandchildren can be halved). Good.

Let's think about if there is any issue with `res1 += curr[0]` when parent halved: child cannot be halved, so we add child's not-halved savings. But if child not halved, child's own discount is 0, but grandchildren can be halved. `curr[0]` includes grandchildren. Good.

Potential issue: If parent halved, can grandchild be halved? Constraint: no two adjacent nodes both halved. Parent halved, child not halved, grandchild halved: parent and grandchild are not adjacent (distance 2), so allowed. Good.

Potential issue: If parent not halved, child halved, grandchild not halved: allowed. `res0 += max(curr)` can choose child halved. Good.

Potential issue: If `trips` contains a trip where start and end are the same, our freq update counts node once. Good.

Now, if I output savings DP, the final code is:

```
from typing import List

class Solution:
    def minimumTotalPrice(self, n: int, edges: List[List[int]], price: List[int], trips: List[List[int]]) -> int:
        g = [[] for _ in range(n)]
        for u, v in edges:
            g[u].append(v)
            g[v].append(u)

        freq = [0] * n
        level = [0] * n
        parent = [0] * n

        def dfs(u, l, p):
            level[u] = l
            parent[u] = p
            for v in g[u]:
                if v != p:
                    dfs(v, l + 1, u)

        def lca(a, b):
            if level[a] > level[b]:
                a, b = b, a
            d = level[b] - level[a]
            while d:
                b = parent[b]
                d -= 1
            if a == b:
                return a
            while a != b:
                a = parent[a]
                b = parent[b]
            return a

        dfs(0, 0, -1)

        for a, b in trips:
            c = lca(a, b)
            x = a
            while x != c:
                freq[x] += 1
                x = parent[x]
            freq[c] += 1
            y = b
            while y != c:
                freq[y] += 1
                y = parent[y]

        def dp(u, p):
            res0 = 0
            res1 = (price[u] - price[u] // 2) * freq[u]
            for v in g[u]:
                if v != p:
                    curr = dp(v, u)
                    res0 += max(curr)
                    res1 += curr[0]
            return [res0, res1]

        ans = 0
        for i in range(n):
            ans += freq[i] * price[i]
        return ans - max(dp(0, -1))
```

```
from typing import List

class Solution:
    def minimumTotalPrice(self, n: int, edges: List[List[int]], price: List[int], trips: List[List[int]]) -> int:
        g = [[] for _ in range(n)]
        for u, v in edges:
            g[u].append(v)
            g[v].append(u)

        freq = [0] * n
        level = [0] * n
        parent = [0] * n

        def dfs(u, l, p):
            level[u] = l
            parent[u] = p
            for v in g[u]:
                if v != p:
                    dfs(v, l + 1, u)

        def lca(a, b):
            if level[a] > level[b]:
                a, b = b, a
            d = level[b] - level[a]
            while d:
                b = parent[b]
                d -= 1
            if a == b:
                return a
            while a != b:
                a = parent[a]
                b = parent[b]
            return a

        dfs(0, 0, -1)

        for a, b in trips:
            c = lca(a, b)
            x = a
            while x != c:
                freq[x] += 1
                x = parent[x]
            freq[c] += 1
            y = b
            while y != c:
                freq[y] += 1
                y = parent[y]

        def dp(u, p):
            res0 = 0
            res1 = (price[u] - price[u] // 2) * freq[u]
            for v in g[u]:
                if v != p:
                    curr = dp(v, u)
                    res0 += max(curr)
                    res1 += curr[0]
            return [res0, res1]

        ans = 0
        for i in range(n):
            ans += freq[i] * price[i]
        return ans - max(dp(0, -1))
```

```
from typing import List

class Solution:
    def minimumTotalPrice(self, n: int, edges: List[List[int]], price: List[int], trips: List[List[int]]) -> int:
        g = [[] for _ in range(n)]
        for u, v in edges:
            g[u].append(v)
            g[v].append(u)

        freq = [0] * n
        level = [0] * n
        parent = [0] * n

        def dfs(u, l, p):
            level[u] = l
            parent[u] = p
            for v in g[u]:
                if v != p:
                    dfs(v, l + 1, u)

        def lca(a, b):
            if level[a] > level[b]:
                a, b = b, a
            d = level[b] - level[a]
            while d:
                b = parent[b]
                d -= 1
            if a == b:
                return a
            while a != b:
                a = parent[a]
                b = parent[b]
            return a

        dfs(0, 0, -1)

        for a, b in trips:
            c = lca(a, b)
            x = a
            while x != c:
                freq[x] += 1
                x = parent[x]
            freq[c] += 1
            y = b
            while y != c:
                freq[y] += 1
                y = parent[y]

        def dp(u, p):
            res0 = 0
            res1 = (price[u] - price[u] // 2) * freq[u]
            for v in g[u]:
                if v != p:
                    curr = dp(v, u)
                    res0 += max(curr)
                    res1 += curr[0]
            return [res0, res1]

        ans = 0
        for i in range(n):
            ans += freq[i] * price[i]
        return ans - max(dp(0, -1))
```

Potential issue: If parent halved, can grandchild be halved? Constraint: no two adjacent nodes both halved. Parent halved, child not halved, grandchild halved: parent and grandchild are not adjacent (distance 2), so allowed. Good.

Potential issue: If parent not halved, child halved, grandchild not halved: allowed. `res0 += max(curr)` can choose child halved. Good.

Now, let's think about if there is any issue with the LCA function's name `lca` and variable `c`. No.

Potential issue: If `trips` contains a trip where start/end are not in tree? no.

Now, if I output savings DP, the final code is:

```
from typing import List

class Solution:
    def minimumTotalPrice(self, n: int, edges: List[List[int]], price: List[int], trips: List[List[int]]) -> int:
        g = [[] for _ in range(n)]
        for u, v in edges:
            g[u].append(v)
            g[v].append(u)

        freq = [0] * n
        level = [0] * n
        parent = [0] * n

        def dfs(u, l, p):
            level[u] = l
            parent[u] = p
            for v in g[u]:
                if v != p:
                    dfs(v, l + 1, u)

        def lca(a, b):
            if level[a] > level[b]:
                a, b = b, a
            d = level[b] - level[a]
            while d:
                b = parent[b]
                d -= 1
            if a == b:
                return a
            while a != b:
                a = parent[a]
                b = parent[b]
            return a

        dfs(0, 0, -1)

        for a, b in trips:
            c = lca(a, b)
            x = a
            while x != c:
                freq[x] += 1
                x = parent[x]
            freq[c] += 1
            y = b
            while y != c:
                freq[y] += 1
                y = parent[y]

        def dp(u, p):
            res0 = 0
            res1 = (price[u] - price[u] // 2) * freq[u]
            for v in g[u]:
                if v != p:
                    curr = dp(v, u)
                    res0 += max(curr)
                    res1 += curr[0]
            return [res0, res1]

        ans = 0
        for i in range(n):
            ans += freq[i] * price[i]
        return ans - max(dp(0, -1))
```

Let's double-check with example 1 using code:
- `dfs` root 0, parent[1]=0, parent[2]=1, parent[3]=1.
- trips:
  [0,3]: lca(0,3): level0=0, level3=2, d=2, b=parent[3]=1, d=1, b=parent[1]=0, d=0, a==b return0. x=0 while x!=0 none, freq[0]+=1. y=3 while y!=0: freq[3]+=1 y=1; freq[1]+=1 y=0. Good.
  [2,1]: lca(2,1): level2=2, level1=1, swap -> a=1,b=2, d=1, b=parent[2]=1, a==b return1. x=2 while x!=1: freq[2]+=1 x=1; freq[1]+=1. y=1 while y!=1 none. Good.
  [2,3]: lca(2,3): level2=2, level3=2, d=0, a!=b, a=parent[2]=1, b=parent[3]=1, return1. x=2 freq2+1 x=1; freq1+1; y=3 freq3+1 y=1. Good.
freq [1,3,2,2]. Good.

- node2 leaf: res0=0, res1=(10-5)*2=10.
- node3 leaf: res0=0, res1=(6-3)*2=6.

- node0: res0=max(16,3)=16, res1=(2-1)*1=1 + curr0(node1)=16 =17.
ans=40, max=17, return23. Good.

```
from typing import List

class Solution:
    def minimumTotalPrice(self, n: int, edges: List[List[int]], price: List[int], trips: List[List[int]]) -> int:
        g = [[] for _ in range(n)]
        for u, v in edges:
            g[u].append(v)
            g[v].append(u)

        freq = [0] * n
        level = [0] * n
        parent = [0] * n

        def dfs(u, l, p):
            level[u] = l
            parent[u] = p
            for v in g[u]:
                if v != p:
                    dfs(v, l + 1, u)

        def lca(a, b):
            if level[a] > level[b]:
                a, b = b, a
            d = level[b] - level[a]
            while d:
                b = parent[b]
                d -= 1
            if a == b:
                return a
            while a != b:
                a = parent[a]
                b = parent[b]
            return a

        dfs(0, 0, -1)

        for a, b in trips:
            c = lca(a, b)
            x = a
            while x != c:
                freq[x] += 1
                x = parent[x]
            freq[c] += 1
            y = b
            while y != c:
                freq[y] += 1
                y = parent[y]

        def dp(u, p):
            res0 = 0
            res1 = (price[u] - price[u] // 2) * freq[u]
            for v in g[u]:
                if v != p:
                    curr = dp(v, u)
                    res0 += max(curr)
                    res1 += curr[0]
            return [res0, res1]

        ans = 0
        for i in range(n):
            ans += freq[i] * price[i]
        return ans - max(dp(0, -1))
```

from typing import List

class Solution:
    def minimumTotalPrice(self, n: int, edges: List[List[int]], price: List[int], trips: List[List[int]]) -> int:
        g = [[] for _ in range(n)]
        for u, v in edges:
            g[u].append(v)
            g[v].append(u)

        freq = [0] * n
        level = [0] * n
        parent = [0] * n

        def dfs(u, l, p):
            level[u] = l
            parent[u] = p
            for v in g[u]:
                if v != p:
                    dfs(v, l + 1, u)

        def lca(a, b):
            if level[a] > level[b]:
                a, b = b, a
            d = level[b] - level[a]
            while d:
                b = parent[b]
                d -= 1
            if a == b:
                return a
            while a != b:
                a = parent[a]
                b = parent[b]
            return a

        dfs(0, 0, -1)

        for a, b in trips:
            c = lca(a, b)
            x = a
            while x != c:
                freq[x] += 1
                x = parent[x]
            freq[c] += 1
            y = b
            while y != c:
                freq[y] += 1
                y = parent[y]

        def dp(u, p):
            res0 = 0
            res1 = (price[u] - price[u] // 2) * freq[u]
            for v in g[u]:
                if v != p:
                    curr = dp(v, u)
                    res0 += max(curr)
                    res1 += curr[0]
            return [res0, res1]

        ans = 0
        for i in range(n):
            ans += freq[i] * price[i]
        return ans - max(dp(0, -1))

```
from typing import List

class Solution:
    def minimumTotalPrice(self, n: int, edges: List[List[int]], price: List[int], trips: List[List[int]]) -> int:
        g = [[] for _ in range(n)]
        for u, v in edges:
            g[u].append(v)
            g[v].append(u)

        freq = [0] * n
        level = [0] * n
        parent = [0] * n

        def dfs(u, l, p):
            level[u] = l
            parent[u] = p
            for v in g[u]:
                if v != p:
                    dfs(v, l + 1, u)

        def lca(a, b):
            if level[a] > level[b]:
                a, b = b, a
            d = level[b] - level[a]
            while d:
                b = parent[b]
                d -= 1
            if a == b:
                return a
            while a != b:
                a = parent[a]
                b = parent[b]
            return a

        dfs(0, 0, -1)

        for a, b in trips:
            c = lca(a, b)
            x = a
            while x != c:
                freq[x] += 1
                x = parent[x]
            freq[c] += 1
            y = b
            while y != c:
                freq[y] += 1
                y = parent[y]

        def dp(u, p):
            res0 = 0
            res1 = (price[u] - price[u] // 2) * freq[u]
            for v in g[u]:
                if v != p:
                    curr = dp(v, u)
                    res0 += max(curr)
                    res1 += curr[0]
            return [res0, res1]

        ans = 0
        for i in range(n):
            ans += freq[i] * price[i]
        return ans - max(dp(0, -1))
```

Let's double-check with example 1 using code:

Let's think about if there is any issue with `res1 += curr[0]` when parent halved: child cannot be halved, so we add child's not-halved savings. But if child not halved, child's own discount is 0, but grandchildren can be halved. `curr[0]` includes grandchildren. Good.

Potential issue: If parent not halved, child halved, grandchild not halved: allowed. `res0 += max(curr)` can choose child halved. Good.

Now, if I output savings DP, the final code is:

```
from typing import List

class Solution:
    def minimumTotalPrice(self, n: int, edges: List[List[int]], price: List[int], trips: List[List[int]]) -> int:
        g = [[] for _ in range(n)]
        for u, v in edges:
            g[u].append(v)
            g[v].append(u)

        freq = [0] * n
        level = [0] * n
        parent = [0] * n

        def dfs(u, l, p):
            level[u] = l
            parent[u] = p
            for v in g[u]:
                if v != p:
                    dfs(v, l + 1, u)

        def lca(a, b):
            if level[a] > level[b]:
                a, b = b, a
            d = level[b] - level[a]
            while d:
                b = parent[b]
                d -= 1
            if a == b:
                return a
            while a != b:
                a = parent[a]
                b = parent[b]
            return a

        dfs(0, 0, -1)

        for a, b in trips:
            c = lca(a, b)
            x = a
            while x != c:
                freq[x] += 1
                x = parent[x]
            freq[c] += 1
            y = b
            while y != c:
                freq[y] += 1
                y = parent[y]

        def dp(u, p):
            res0 = 0
            res1 = (price[u] - price[u] // 2) * freq[u]
            for v in g[u]:
                if v != p:
                    curr = dp(v, u)
                    res0 += max(curr)
                    res1 += curr[0]
            return [res0, res1]

        ans = 0
        for i in range(n):
            ans += freq[i] * price[i]
        return ans - max(dp(0, -1))
```

Let's double-check with example 1 using code:

  [0,3]: lca(0,3): level0=0, level3=2, d=2, b=parent[3]=1, d=1, b=parent[1]=0, d=0, a==b return0. x=0 while x!=0 none, freq[0]+=1. y=3 while y!=0: freq[3]+=1 y=1; freq[1]+=1 y=0. Good.
  [2,1]: lca(2,1): level2=2, level1=1, swap -> a=1,b=2, d=1, b=parent[2]=1, a==b return1. x=2 while x!=1: freq[2]+=1 x=1; freq[1]+=1. y=1 while y!=1 none. Good.
  [2,3]: lca(2,3): level2=2, level3=2, d=0, a!=b, a=parent[2]=1, b=parent[3]=1, return1. x=2 freq2+1 x=1; freq1+1; y=3 freq3+1 y=1. Good.
freq [1,3,2,2]. Good.

```
from typing import List

class Solution:
    def minimumTotalPrice(self, n: int, edges: List[List[int]], price: List[int], trips: List[List[int]]) -> int:
        g = [[] for _ in range(n)]
        for u, v in edges:
            g[u].append(v)
            g[v].append(u)

        freq = [0] * n
        level = [0] * n
        parent = [0] * n

        def dfs(u, l, p):
            level[u] = l
            parent[u] = p
            for v in g[u]:
                if v != p:
                    dfs(v, l + 1, u)

        def lca(a, b):
            if level[a] > level[b]:
                a, b = b, a
            d = level[b] - level[a]
            while d:
                b = parent[b]
                d -= 1
            if a == b:
                return a
            while a != b:
                a = parent[a]
                b = parent[b]
            return a

        dfs(0, 0, -1)

        for a, b in trips:
            c = lca(a, b)
            x = a
            while x != c:
                freq[x] += 1
                x = parent[x]
            freq[c] += 1
            y = b
            while y != c:
                freq[y] += 1
                y = parent[y]

        def dp(u, p):
            res0 = 0
            res1 = (price[u] - price[u] // 2) * freq[u]
            for v in g[u]:
                if v != p:
                    curr = dp(v, u)
                    res0 += max(curr)
                    res1 += curr[0]
            return [res0, res1]

        ans = 0
        for i in range(n):
            ans += freq[i] * price[i]
        return ans - max(dp(0, -1))
```

```
from typing import List

class Solution:
    def minimumTotalPrice(self, n: int, edges: List[List[int]], price: List[int], trips: List[List[int]]) -> int:
        g = [[] for _ in range(n)]
        for u, v in edges:
            g[u].append(v)
            g[v].append(u)

        freq = [0] * n
        level = [0] * n
        parent = [0] * n

        def dfs(u, l, p):
            level[u] = l
            parent[u] = p
            for v in g[u]:
                if v != p:
                    dfs(v, l + 1, u)

        def lca(a, b):
            if level[a] > level[b]:
                a, b = b, a
            d = level[b] - level[a]
            while d:
                b = parent[b]
                d -= 1
            if a == b:
                return a
            while a != b:
                a = parent[a]
                b = parent[b]
            return a

        dfs(0, 0, -1)

        for a, b in trips:
            c = lca(a, b)
            x = a
            while x != c:
                freq[x] += 1
                x = parent[x]
            freq[c] += 1
            y = b
            while y != c:
                freq[y] += 1
                y = parent[y]

        def dp(u, p):
            res0 = 0
            res1 = (price[u] - price[u] // 2) * freq[u]
            for v in g[u]:
                if v != p:
                    curr = dp(v, u)
                    res0 += max(curr)
                    res1 += curr[0]
            return [res0, res1]

        ans = 0
        for i in range(n):
            ans += freq[i] * price[i]
        return ans - max(dp(0, -1))
```

Now, if I output savings DP, the final code is:

```
from typing import List

class Solution:
    def minimumTotalPrice(self, n: int, edges: List[List[int]], price: List[int], trips: List[List[int]]) -> int:
        g = [[] for _ in range(n)]
        for u, v in edges:
            g[u].append(v)
            g[v].append(u)

        freq = [0] * n
        level = [0] * n
        parent = [0] * n

        def dfs(u, l, p):
            level[u] = l
            parent[u] = p
            for v in g[u]:
                if v != p:
                    dfs(v, l + 1, u)

        def lca(a, b):
            if level[a] > level[b]:
                a, b = b, a
            d = level[b] - level[a]
            while d:
                b = parent[b]
                d -= 1
            if a == b:
                return a
            while a != b:
                a = parent[a]
                b = parent[b]
            return a

        dfs(0, 0, -1)

        for a, b in trips:
            c = lca(a, b)
            x = a
            while x != c:
                freq[x] += 1
                x = parent[x]
            freq[c] += 1
            y = b
            while y != c:
                freq[y] += 1
                y = parent[y]

        def dp(u, p):
            res0 = 0
            res1 = (price[u] - price[u] // 2) * freq[u]
            for v in g[u]:
                if v != p:
                    curr = dp(v, u)
                    res0 += max(curr)
                    res1 += curr[0]
            return [res0, res1]

        ans = 0
        for i in range(n):
            ans += freq[i] * price[i]
        return ans - max(dp(0, -1))
```

Let's double-check with example 1 using code:
- `dfs` root 0, parent[1]=0, parent[2]=1, parent[3]=1.
- trips:
  [0,3]: lca(0,3): level0=0, level3=2, d=2, b=parent[3]=1, d=1, b=parent[1]=0, d=0, a==b return0. x=0 while x!=0 none, freq[0]+=1. y=3 while y!=0: freq[3]+=1 y=1; freq[1]+=1 y=0. Good.
  [2,1]: lca(2,1): level2=2, level1=1, swap -> a=1,b=2, d=1, b=parent[2]=1, a==b return1. x=2 while x!=1: freq[2]+=1 x=1; freq[1]+=1. y=1 while y!=1 none. Good.
  [2,3]: lca(2,3): level2=2, level3=2, d=0, a!=b, a=parent[2]=1, b=parent[3]=1, return1. x=2 freq2+1 x=1; freq1+1; y=3 freq3+1 y=1. Good.
freq [1,3,2,2]. Good.

DP:
- node2 leaf: res0=0, res1=(10-5)*2=10.
- node3 leaf: res0=0, res1=(6-3)*2=6.
- node1: res0=max(0,10)+max(0,6)=16, res1=(2-1)*3=3 + curr0(node2)=0 + curr0(node3)=0 =3.
- node0: res0=max(16,3)=16, res1=(2-1)*1=1 + curr0(node1)=16 =17.
ans=40, max=17, return23. Good.

```
from typing import List

class Solution:
    def minimumTotalPrice(self, n: int, edges: List[List[int]], price: List[int], trips: List[List[int]]) -> int:
        g = [[] for _ in range(n)]
        for u, v in edges:
            g[u].append(v)
            g[v].append(u)

        freq = [0] * n
        level = [0] * n
        parent = [0] * n

        def dfs(u, l, p):
            level[u] = l
            parent[u] = p
            for v in g[u]:
                if v != p:
                    dfs(v, l + 1, u)

        def lca(a, b):
            if level[a] > level[b]:
                a, b = b, a
            d = level[b] - level[a]
            while d:
                b = parent[b]
                d -= 1
            if a == b:
                return a
            while a != b:
                a = parent[a]
                b = parent[b]
            return a

        dfs(0, 0, -1)

        for a, b in trips:
            c = lca(a, b)
            x = a
            while x != c:
                freq[x] += 1
                x = parent[x]
            freq[c] += 1
            y = b
            while y != c:
                freq[y] += 1
                y = parent[y]

        def dp(u, p):
            res0 = 0
            res1 = (price[u] - price[u] // 2) * freq[u]
            for v in g[u]:
                if v != p:
                    curr = dp(v, u)
                    res0 += max(curr)
                    res1 += curr[0]
            return [res0, res1]

        ans = 0
        for i in range(n):
            ans += freq[i] * price[i]
        return ans - max(dp(0, -1))
```

from typing import List

class Solution:
    def minimumTotalPrice(self, n: int, edges: List[List[int]], price: List[int], trips: List[List[int]]) -> int:
        g = [[] for _ in range(n)]
        for u, v in edges:
            g[u].append(v)
            g[v].append(u)

        freq = [0] * n
        level = [0] * n
        parent = [0] * n

        def dfs(u, l, p):
            level[u] = l
            parent[u] = p
            for v in g[u]:
                if v != p:
                    dfs(v, l + 1, u)

        def lca(a, b):
            if level[a] > level[b]:
                a, b = b, a
            d = level[b] - level[a]
            while d:
                b = parent[b]
                d -= 1
            if a == b:
                return a
            while a != b:
                a = parent[a]
                b = parent[b]
            return a

        dfs(0, 0, -1)

        for a, b in trips:
            c = lca(a, b)
            x = a
            while x != c:
                freq[x] += 1
                x = parent[x]
            freq[c] += 1
            y = b
            while y != c:
                freq[y] += 1
                y = parent[y]

        def dp(u, p):
            res0 = 0
            res1 = (price[u] - price[u] // 2) * freq[u]
            for v in g[u]:
                if v != p:
                    curr = dp(v, u)
                    res0 += max(curr)
                    res1 += curr[0]
            return [res0, res1]

        ans = 0
        for i in range(n):
            ans += freq[i] * price[i]
        return ans - max(dp(0, -1))
```
