```
from typing import List

class Solution:
    def minimumTotalPrice(self, n: int, edges: List[List[int]], price: List[int], trips: List[List[int]]) -> int:
        g = [[] for _ in range(n)]
        for u, v in edges:
            g[u].append(v)
            g[v].append(u)

        freq = [0] * n
        level = [0] * n
        parent = [0] * n

        def dfs(u, l, p):
            level[u] = l
            parent[u] = p
            for v in g[u]:
                if v != p:
                    dfs(v, l + 1, u)

        def lca(a, b):
            if level[a] > level[b]:
                a, b = b, a
            d = level[b] - level[a]
            while d:
                b = parent[b]
                d -= 1
            if a == b:
                return a
            while a != b:
                a = parent[a]
                b = parent[b]
            return a

        dfs(0, 0, -1)

        for a, b in trips:
            c = lca(a, b)
            x = a
            while x != c:
                freq[x] += 1
                x = parent[x]
            freq[c] += 1
            y = b
            while y != c:
                freq[y] += 1
                y = parent[y]

        def dp(u, p):
            res0 = 0
            res1 = (price[u] - price[u] // 2) * freq[u]
            for v in g[u]:
                if v != p:
                    curr = dp(v, u)
                    res0 += max(curr)
                    res1 += curr[0]
            return [res0, res1]

        ans = 0
        for i in range(n):
            ans += freq[i] * price[i]
        return ans - max(dp(0, -1))
```

- node1: res0=max(0,10)+max(0,6)=16, res1=(2-1)*3=3 + curr0(node2)=0 + curr0(node3)=0 =3.
- node0: res0=max(16,3)=16, res1=(2-1)*1=1 + curr0(node1)=16 =17.
ans=40, max=17, return23. Good.

Now, if I output savings DP, the final code is:

```
from typing import List

class Solution:
    def minimumTotalPrice(self, n: int, edges: List[List[int]], price: List[int], trips: List[List[int]]) -> int:
        g = [[] for _ in range(n)]
        for u, v in edges:
            g[u].append(v)
            g[v].append(u)

        freq = [0] * n
        level = [0] * n
        parent = [0] * n

        def dfs(u, l, p):
            level[u] = l
            parent[u] = p
            for v in g[u]:
                if v != p:
                    dfs(v, l + 1, u)

        def lca(a, b):
            if level[a] > level[b]:
                a, b = b, a
            d = level[b] - level[a]
            while d:
                b = parent[b]
                d -= 1
            if a == b:
                return a
            while a != b:
                a = parent[a]
                b = parent[b]
            return a

        dfs(0, 0, -1)

        for a, b in trips:
            c = lca(a, b)
            x = a
            while x != c:
                freq[x] += 1
                x = parent[x]
            freq[c] += 1
            y = b
            while y != c:
                freq[y] += 1
                y = parent[y]

        def dp(u, p):
            res0 = 0
            res1 = (price[u] - price[u] // 2) * freq[u]
            for v in g[u]:
                if v != p:
                    curr = dp(v, u)
                    res0 += max(curr)
                    res1 += curr[0]
            return [res0, res1]

        ans = 0
        for i in range(n):
            ans += freq[i] * price[i]
        return ans - max(dp(0, -1))
```

Let's double-check with example 1 using code:
- `dfs` root 0, parent[1]=0, parent[2]=1, parent[3]=1.
- trips:
  [0,3]: lca(0,3): level0=0, level3=2, d=2, b=parent[3]=1, d=1, b=parent[1]=0, d=0, a==b return0. x=0 while x!=0 none, freq[0]+=1. y=3 while y!=0: freq[3]+=1 y=1; freq[1]+=1 y=0. Good.
  [2,1]: lca(2,1): level2=2, level1=1, swap -> a=1,b=2, d=1, b=parent[2]=1, a==b return1. x=2 while x!=1: freq[2]+=1 x=1; freq[1]+=1. y=1 while y!=1 none. Good.
  [2,3]: lca(2,3): level2=2, level3=2, d=0, a!=b, a=parent[2]=1, b=parent[3]=1, return1. x=2 freq2+1 x=1; freq1+1; y=3 freq3+1 y=1. Good.
freq [1,3,2,2]. Good.

```
from typing import List

class Solution:
    def minimumTotalPrice(self, n: int, edges: List[List[int]], price: List[int], trips: List[List[int]]) -> int:
        g = [[] for _ in range(n)]
        for u, v in edges:
            g[u].append(v)
            g[v].append(u)

        freq = [0] * n
        level = [0] * n
        parent = [0] * n

        def dfs(u, l, p):
            level[u] = l
            parent[u] = p
            for v in g[u]:
                if v != p:
                    dfs(v, l + 1, u)

        def lca(a, b):
            if level[a] > level[b]:
                a, b = b, a
            d = level[b] - level[a]
            while d:
                b = parent[b]
                d -= 1
            if a == b:
                return a
            while a != b:
                a = parent[a]
                b = parent[b]
            return a

        dfs(0, 0, -1)

        for a, b in trips:
            c = lca(a, b)
            x = a
            while x != c:
                freq[x] += 1
                x = parent[x]
            freq[c] += 1
            y = b
            while y != c:
                freq[y] += 1
                y = parent[y]

        def dp(u, p):
            res0 = 0
            res1 = (price[u] - price[u] // 2) * freq[u]
            for v in g[u]:
                if v != p:
                    curr = dp(v, u)
                    res0 += max(curr)
                    res1 += curr[0]
            return [res0, res1]

        ans = 0
        for i in range(n):
            ans += freq[i] * price[i]
        return ans - max(dp(0, -1))
```

```
from typing import List

class Solution:
    def minimumTotalPrice(self, n: int, edges: List[List[int]], price: List[int], trips: List[List[int]]) -> int:
        g = [[] for _ in range(n)]
        for u, v in edges:
            g[u].append(v)
            g[v].append(u)

        freq = [0] * n
        level = [0] * n
        parent = [0] * n

        def dfs(u, l, p):
            level[u] = l
            parent[u] = p
            for v in g[u]:
                if v != p:
                    dfs(v, l + 1, u)

        def lca(a, b):
            if level[a] > level[b]:
                a, b = b, a
            d = level[b] - level[a]
            while d:
                b = parent[b]
                d -= 1
            if a == b:
                return a
            while a != b:
                a = parent[a]
                b = parent[b]
            return a

        dfs(0, 0, -1)

        for a, b in trips:
            c = lca(a, b)
            x = a
            while x != c:
                freq[x] += 1
                x = parent[x]
            freq[c] += 1
            y = b
            while y != c:
                freq[y] += 1
                y = parent[y]

        def dp(u, p):
            res0 = 0
            res1 = (price[u] - price[u] // 2) * freq[u]
            for v in g[u]:
                if v != p:
                    curr = dp(v, u)
                    res0 += max(curr)
                    res1 += curr[0]
            return [res0, res1]

        ans = 0
        for i in range(n):
            ans += freq[i] * price[i]
        return ans - max(dp(0, -1))
```

from typing import List

class Solution:
    def minimumTotalPrice(self, n: int, edges: List[List[int]], price: List[int], trips: List[List[int]]) -> int:
        g = [[] for _ in range(n)]
        for u, v in edges:
            g[u].append(v)
            g[v].append(u)

        freq = [0] * n
        level = [0] * n
        parent = [0] * n

        def dfs(u, l, p):
            level[u] = l
            parent[u] = p
            for v in g[u]:
                if v != p:
                    dfs(v, l + 1, u)

        def lca(a, b):
            if level[a] > level[b]:
                a, b = b, a
            d = level[b] - level[a]
            while d:
                b = parent[b]
                d -= 1
            if a == b:
                return a
            while a != b:
                a = parent[a]
                b = parent[b]
            return a

        dfs(0, 0, -1)

        for a, b in trips:
            c = lca(a, b)
            x = a
            while x != c:
                freq[x] += 1
                x = parent[x]
            freq[c] += 1
            y = b
            while y != c:
                freq[y] += 1
                y = parent[y]

        def dp(u, p):
            res0 = 0
            res1 = (price[u] - price[u] // 2) * freq[u]
            for v in g[u]:
                if v != p:
                    curr = dp(v, u)
                    res0 += max(curr)
                    res1 += curr[0]
            return [res0, res1]

        ans = 0
        for i in range(n):
            ans += freq[i] * price[i]
        return ans - max(dp(0, -1))
```

Let's double-check with example 1 using code:
- `dfs` root 0, parent[1]=0, parent[2]=1, parent[3]=1.
- trips:
  [0,3]: lca(0,3): level0=0, level3=2, d=2, b=parent[3]=1, d=1, b=parent[1]=0, d=0, a==b return0. x=0 while x!=0 none, freq[0]+=1. y=3 while y!=0: freq[3]+=1 y=1; freq[1]+=1 y=0. Good.
  [2,1]: lca(2,1): level2=2, level1=1, swap -> a=1,b=2, d=1, b=parent[2]=1, a==b return1. x=2 while x!=1: freq[2]+=1 x=1; freq[1]+=1. y=1 while y!=1 none. Good.
  [2,3]: lca(2,3): level2=2, level3=2, d=0, a!=b, a=parent[2]=1, b=parent[3]=1, return1. x=2 freq2+1 x=1; freq1+1; y=3 freq3+1 y=1. Good.
freq [1,3,2,2]. Good.

- node2 leaf: res0=0, res1=(10-5)*2=10.
- node3 leaf: res0=0, res1=(6-3)*2=6.
- node1: res0=max(0,10)+max(0,6)=16, res1=(2-1)*3=3 + curr0(node2)=0 + curr0(node3)=0 =3.
- node0: res0=max(16,3)=16, res1=(2-1)*1=1 + curr0(node1)=16 =17.

Let's think about if there is any issue with `res1 += curr[0]` when parent halved: child cannot be halved, so we add child's not-halved savings. But if child not halved, child's own discount is 0, but grandchildren can be halved. `curr[0]` includes grandchildren. Good.

Potential issue: If parent halved, can grandchild be halved? Constraint: no two adjacent nodes both halved. Parent halved, child not halved, grandchild halved: parent and grandchild are not adjacent (distance 2), so allowed. Good.

Potential issue: If parent not halved, child halved, grandchild not halved: allowed. `res0 += max(curr)` can choose child halved. Good.

Now, if I output savings DP, the final code is:

```
from typing import List

class Solution:
    def minimumTotalPrice(self, n: int, edges: List[List[int]], price: List[int], trips: List[List[int]]) -> int:
        g = [[] for _ in range(n)]
        for u, v in edges:
            g[u].append(v)
            g[v].append(u)

        freq = [0] * n
        level = [0] * n
        parent = [0] * n

        def dfs(u, l, p):
            level[u] = l
            parent[u] = p
            for v in g[u]:
                if v != p:
                    dfs(v, l + 1, u)

        def lca(a, b):
            if level[a] > level[b]:
                a, b = b, a
            d = level[b] - level[a]
            while d:
                b = parent[b]
                d -= 1
            if a == b:
                return a
            while a != b:
                a = parent[a]
                b = parent[b]
            return a

        dfs(0, 0, -1)

        for a, b in trips:
            c = lca(a, b)
            x = a
            while x != c:
                freq[x] += 1
                x = parent[x]
            freq[c] += 1
            y = b
            while y != c:
                freq[y] += 1
                y = parent[y]

        def dp(u, p):
            res0 = 0
            res1 = (price[u] - price[u] // 2) * freq[u]
            for v in g[u]:
                if v != p:
                    curr = dp(v, u)
                    res0 += max(curr)
                    res1 += curr[0]
            return [res0, res1]

        ans = 0
        for i in range(n):
            ans += freq[i] * price[i]
        return ans - max(dp(0, -1))
```

```
from typing import List

class Solution:
    def minimumTotalPrice(self, n: int, edges: List[List[int]], price: List[int], trips: List[List[int]]) -> int:
        g = [[] for _ in range(n)]
        for u, v in edges:
            g[u].append(v)
            g[v].append(u)

        freq = [0] * n
        level = [0] * n
        parent = [0] * n

        def dfs(u, l, p):
            level[u] = l
            parent[u] = p
            for v in g[u]:
                if v != p:
                    dfs(v, l + 1, u)

        def lca(a, b):
            if level[a] > level[b]:
                a, b = b, a
            d = level[b] - level[a]
            while d:
                b = parent[b]
                d -= 1
            if a == b:
                return a
            while a != b:
                a = parent[a]
                b = parent[b]
            return a

        dfs(0, 0, -1)

        for a, b in trips:
            c = lca(a, b)
            x = a
            while x != c:
                freq[x] += 1
                x = parent[x]
            freq[c] += 1
            y = b
            while y != c:
                freq[y] += 1
                y = parent[y]

        def dp(u, p):
            res0 = 0
            res1 = (price[u] - price[u] // 2) * freq[u]
            for v in g[u]:
                if v != p:
                    curr = dp(v, u)
                    res0 += max(curr)
                    res1 += curr[0]
            return [res0, res1]

        ans = 0
        for i in range(n):
            ans += freq[i] * price[i]
        return ans - max(dp(0, -1))
```

```
from typing import List

class Solution:
    def minimumTotalPrice(self, n: int, edges: List[List[int]], price: List[int], trips: List[List[int]]) -> int:
        g = [[] for _ in range(n)]
        for u, v in edges:
            g[u].append(v)
            g[v].append(u)

        freq = [0] * n
        level = [0] * n
        parent = [0] * n

        def dfs(u, l, p):
            level[u] = l
            parent[u] = p
            for v in g[u]:
                if v != p:
                    dfs(v, l + 1, u)

        def lca(a, b):
            if level[a] > level[b]:
                a, b = b, a
            d = level[b] - level[a]
            while d:
                b = parent[b]
                d -= 1
            if a == b:
                return a
            while a != b:
                a = parent[a]
                b = parent[b]
            return a

        dfs(0, 0, -1)

        for a, b in trips:
            c = lca(a, b)
            x = a
            while x != c:
                freq[x] += 1
                x = parent[x]
            freq[c] += 1
            y = b
            while y != c:
                freq[y] += 1
                y = parent[y]

        def dp(u, p):
            res0 = 0
            res1 = (price[u] - price[u] // 2) * freq[u]
            for v in g[u]:
                if v != p:
                    curr = dp(v, u)
                    res0 += max(curr)
                    res1 += curr[0]
            return [res0, res1]

        ans = 0
        for i in range(n):
            ans += freq[i] * price[i]
        return ans - max(dp(0, -1))
```

Potential issue: If parent halved, can grandchild be halved? Constraint: no two adjacent nodes both halved. Parent halved, child not halved, grandchild halved: parent and grandchild are not adjacent (distance 2), so allowed. Good.

Potential issue: If parent not halved, child halved, grandchild not halved: allowed. `res0 += max(curr)` can choose child halved. Good.

Now, let's think about if there is any issue with the LCA function's name `lca` and variable `c`. No.

Potential issue: If `trips` contains a trip where start/end are not in tree? no.

Now, if I output savings DP, the final code is:

```
from typing import List

class Solution:
    def minimumTotalPrice(self, n: int, edges: List[List[int]], price: List[int], trips: List[List[int]]) -> int:
        g = [[] for _ in range(n)]
        for u, v in edges:
            g[u].append(v)
            g[v].append(u)

        freq = [0] * n
        level = [0] * n
        parent = [0] * n

        def dfs(u, l, p):
            level[u] = l
            parent[u] = p
            for v in g[u]:
                if v != p:
                    dfs(v, l + 1, u)

        def lca(a, b):
            if level[a] > level[b]:
                a, b = b, a
            d = level[b] - level[a]
            while d:
                b = parent[b]
                d -= 1
            if a == b:
                return a
            while a != b:
                a = parent[a]
                b = parent[b]
            return a

        dfs(0, 0, -1)

        for a, b in trips:
            c = lca(a, b)
            x = a
            while x != c:
                freq[x] += 1
                x = parent[x]
            freq[c] += 1
            y = b
            while y != c:
                freq[y] += 1
                y = parent[y]

        def dp(u, p):
            res0 = 0
            res1 = (price[u] - price[u] // 2) * freq[u]
            for v in g[u]:
                if v != p:
                    curr = dp(v, u)
                    res0 += max(curr)
                    res1 += curr[0]
            return [res0, res1]

        ans = 0
        for i in range(n):
            ans += freq[i] * price[i]
        return ans - max(dp(0, -1))
```

Let's double-check with example 1 using code:
- `dfs` root 0, parent[1]=0, parent[2]=1, parent[3]=1.
- trips:
  [0,3]: lca(0,3): level0=0, level3=2, d=2, b=parent[3]=1, d=1, b=parent[1]=0, d=0, a==b return0. x=0 while x!=0 none, freq[0]+=1. y=3 while y!=0: freq[3]+=1 y=1; freq[1]+=1 y=0. Good.
  [2,1]: lca(2,1): level2=2, level1=1, swap -> a=1,b=2, d=1, b=parent[2]=1, a==b return1. x=2 while x!=1: freq[2]+=1 x=1; freq[1]+=1. y=1 while y!=1 none. Good.
  [2,3]: lca(2,3): level2=2, level3=2, d=0, a!=b, a=parent[2]=1, b=parent[3]=1, return1. x=2 freq2+1 x=1; freq1+1; y=3 freq3+1 y=1. Good.
freq [1,3,2,2]. Good.

- node2 leaf: res0=0, res1=(10-5)*2=10.
- node3 leaf: res0=0, res1=(6-3)*2=6.

- node0: res0=max(16,3)=16, res1=(2-1)*1=1 + curr0(node1)=16 =17.
ans=40, max=17, return23. Good.

```
from typing import List

class Solution:
    def minimumTotalPrice(self, n: int, edges: List[List[int]], price: List[int], trips: List[List[int]]) -> int:
        g = [[] for _ in range(n)]
        for u, v in edges:
            g[u].append(v)
            g[v].append(u)

        freq = [0] * n
        level = [0] * n
        parent = [0] * n

        def dfs(u, l, p):
            level[u] = l
            parent[u] = p
            for v in g[u]:
                if v != p:
                    dfs(v, l + 1, u)

        def lca(a, b):
            if level[a] > level[b]:
                a, b = b, a
            d = level[b] - level[a]
            while d:
                b = parent[b]
                d -= 1
            if a == b:
                return a
            while a != b:
                a = parent[a]
                b = parent[b]
            return a

        dfs(0, 0, -1)

        for a, b in trips:
            c = lca(a, b)
            x = a
            while x != c:
                freq[x] += 1
                x = parent[x]
            freq[c] += 1
            y = b
            while y != c:
                freq[y] += 1
                y = parent[y]

        def dp(u, p):
            res0 = 0
            res1 = (price[u] - price[u] // 2) * freq[u]
            for v in g[u]:
                if v != p:
                    curr = dp(v, u)
                    res0 += max(curr)
                    res1 += curr[0]
            return [res0, res1]

        ans = 0
        for i in range(n):
            ans += freq[i] * price[i]
        return ans - max(dp(0, -1))
```

from typing import List

class Solution:
    def minimumTotalPrice(self, n: int, edges: List[List[int]], price: List[int], trips: List[List[int]]) -> int:
        g = [[] for _ in range(n)]
        for u, v in edges:
            g[u].append(v)
            g[v].append(u)

        freq = [0] * n
        level = [0] * n
        parent = [0] * n

        def dfs(u, l, p):
            level[u] = l
            parent[u] = p
            for v in g[u]:
                if v != p:
                    dfs(v, l + 1, u)

        def lca(a, b):
            if level[a] > level[b]:
                a, b = b, a
            d = level[b] - level[a]
            while d:
                b = parent[b]
                d -= 1
            if a == b:
                return a
            while a != b:
                a = parent[a]
                b = parent[b]
            return a

        dfs(0, 0, -1)

        for a, b in trips:
            c = lca(a, b)
            x = a
            while x != c:
                freq[x] += 1
                x = parent[x]
            freq[c] += 1
            y = b
            while y != c:
                freq[y] += 1
                y = parent[y]

        def dp(u, p):
            res0 = 0
            res1 = (price[u] - price[u] // 2) * freq[u]
            for v in g[u]:
                if v != p:
                    curr = dp(v, u)
                    res0 += max(curr)
                    res1 += curr[0]
            return [res0, res1]

        ans = 0
        for i in range(n):
            ans += freq[i] * price[i]
        return ans - max(dp(0, -1))

```
from typing import List

class Solution:
    def minimumTotalPrice(self, n: int, edges: List[List[int]], price: List[int], trips: List[List[int]]) -> int:
        g = [[] for _ in range(n)]
        for u, v in edges:
            g[u].append(v)
            g[v].append(u)

        freq = [0] * n
        level = [0] * n
        parent = [0] * n

        def dfs(u, l, p):
            level[u] = l
            parent[u] = p
            for v in g[u]:
                if v != p:
                    dfs(v, l + 1, u)

        def lca(a, b):
            if level[a] > level[b]:
                a, b = b, a
            d = level[b] - level[a]
            while d:
                b = parent[b]
                d -= 1
            if a == b:
                return a
            while a != b:
                a = parent[a]
                b = parent[b]
            return a

        dfs(0, 0, -1)

        for a, b in trips:
            c = lca(a, b)
            x = a
            while x != c:
                freq[x] += 1
                x = parent[x]
            freq[c] += 1
            y = b
            while y != c:
                freq[y] += 1
                y = parent[y]

        def dp(u, p):
            res0 = 0
            res1 = (price[u] - price[u] // 2) * freq[u]
            for v in g[u]:
                if v != p:
                    curr = dp(v, u)
                    res0 += max(curr)
                    res1 += curr[0]
            return [res0, res1]

        ans = 0
        for i in range(n):
            ans += freq[i] * price[i]
        return ans - max(dp(0, -1))
```

Let's double-check with example 1 using code:

Let's think about if there is any issue with `res1 += curr[0]` when parent halved: child cannot be halved, so we add child's not-halved savings. But if child not halved, child's own discount is 0, but grandchildren can be halved. `curr[0]` includes grandchildren. Good.

Potential issue: If parent not halved, child halved, grandchild not halved: allowed. `res0 += max(curr)` can choose child halved. Good.

Now, if I output savings DP, the final code is:

```
from typing import List

class Solution:
    def minimumTotalPrice(self, n: int, edges: List[List[int]], price: List[int], trips: List[List[int]]) -> int:
        g = [[] for _ in range(n)]
        for u, v in edges:
            g[u].append(v)
            g[v].append(u)

        freq = [0] * n
        level = [0] * n
        parent = [0] * n

        def dfs(u, l, p):
            level[u] = l
            parent[u] = p
            for v in g[u]:
                if v != p:
                    dfs(v, l + 1, u)

        def lca(a, b):
            if level[a] > level[b]:
                a, b = b, a
            d = level[b] - level[a]
            while d:
                b = parent[b]
                d -= 1
            if a == b:
                return a
            while a != b:
                a = parent[a]
                b = parent[b]
            return a

        dfs(0, 0, -1)

        for a, b in trips:
            c = lca(a, b)
            x = a
            while x != c:
                freq[x] += 1
                x = parent[x]
            freq[c] += 1
            y = b
            while y != c:
                freq[y] += 1
                y = parent[y]

        def dp(u, p):
            res0 = 0
            res1 = (price[u] - price[u] // 2) * freq[u]
            for v in g[u]:
                if v != p:
                    curr = dp(v, u)
                    res0 += max(curr)
                    res1 += curr[0]
            return [res0, res1]

        ans = 0
        for i in range(n):
            ans += freq[i] * price[i]
        return ans - max(dp(0, -1))
```

Let's double-check with example 1 using code:

  [0,3]: lca(0,3): level0=0, level3=2, d=2, b=parent[3]=1, d=1, b=parent[1]=0, d=0, a==b return0. x=0 while x!=0 none, freq[0]+=1. y=3 while y!=0: freq[3]+=1 y=1; freq[1]+=1 y=0. Good.
  [2,1]: lca(2,1): level2=2, level1=1, swap -> a=1,b=2, d=1, b=parent[2]=1, a==b return1. x=2 while x!=1: freq[2]+=1 x=1; freq[1]+=1. y=1 while y!=1 none. Good.
  [2,3]: lca(2,3): level2=2, level3=2, d=0, a!=b, a=parent[2]=1, b=parent[3]=1, return1. x=2 freq2+1 x=1; freq1+1; y=3 freq3+1 y=1. Good.
freq [1,3,2,2]. Good.

```
from typing import List

class Solution:
    def minimumTotalPrice(self, n: int, edges: List[List[int]], price: List[int], trips: List[List[int]]) -> int:
        g = [[] for _ in range(n)]
        for u, v in edges:
            g[u].append(v)
            g[v].append(u)

        freq = [0] * n
        level = [0] * n
        parent = [0] * n

        def dfs(u, l, p):
            level[u] = l
            parent[u] = p
            for v in g[u]:
                if v != p:
                    dfs(v, l + 1, u)

        def lca(a, b):
            if level[a] > level[b]:
                a, b = b, a
            d = level[b] - level[a]
            while d:
                b = parent[b]
                d -= 1
            if a == b:
                return a
            while a != b:
                a = parent[a]
                b = parent[b]
            return a

        dfs(0, 0, -1)

        for a, b in trips:
            c = lca(a, b)
            x = a
            while x != c:
                freq[x] += 1
                x = parent[x]
            freq[c] += 1
            y = b
            while y != c:
                freq[y] += 1
                y = parent[y]

        def dp(u, p):
            res0 = 0
            res1 = (price[u] - price[u] // 2) * freq[u]
            for v in g[u]:
                if v != p:
                    curr = dp(v, u)
                    res0 += max(curr)
                    res1 += curr[0]
            return [res0, res1]

        ans = 0
        for i in range(n):
            ans += freq[i] * price[i]
        return ans - max(dp(0, -1))
```

```
from typing import List

class Solution:
    def minimumTotalPrice(self, n: int, edges: List[List[int]], price: List[int], trips: List[List[int]]) -> int:
        g = [[] for _ in range(n)]
        for u, v in edges:
            g[u].append(v)
            g[v].append(u)

        freq = [0] * n
        level = [0] * n
        parent = [0] * n

        def dfs(u, l, p):
            level[u] = l
            parent[u] = p
            for v in g[u]:
                if v != p:
                    dfs(v, l + 1, u)

        def lca(a, b):
            if level[a] > level[b]:
                a, b = b, a
            d = level[b] - level[a]
            while d:
                b = parent[b]
                d -= 1
            if a == b:
                return a
            while a != b:
                a = parent[a]
                b = parent[b]
            return a

        dfs(0, 0, -1)

        for a, b in trips:
            c = lca(a, b)
            x = a
            while x != c:
                freq[x] += 1
                x = parent[x]
            freq[c] += 1
            y = b
            while y != c:
                freq[y] += 1
                y = parent[y]

        def dp(u, p):
            res0 = 0
            res1 = (price[u] - price[u] // 2) * freq[u]
            for v in g[u]:
                if v != p:
                    curr = dp(v, u)
                    res0 += max(curr)
                    res1 += curr[0]
            return [res0, res1]

        ans = 0
        for i in range(n):
            ans += freq[i] * price[i]
        return ans - max(dp(0, -1))
```

Now, if I output savings DP, the final code is:

```
from typing import List

class Solution:
    def minimumTotalPrice(self, n: int, edges: List[List[int]], price: List[int], trips: List[List[int]]) -> int:
        g = [[] for _ in range(n)]
        for u, v in edges:
            g[u].append(v)
            g[v].append(u)

        freq = [0] * n
        level = [0] * n
        parent = [0] * n

        def dfs(u, l, p):
            level[u] = l
            parent[u] = p
            for v in g[u]:
                if v != p:
                    dfs(v, l + 1, u)

        def lca(a, b):
            if level[a] > level[b]:
                a, b = b, a
            d = level[b] - level[a]
            while d:
                b = parent[b]
                d -= 1
            if a == b:
                return a
            while a != b:
                a = parent[a]
                b = parent[b]
            return a

        dfs(0, 0, -1)

        for a, b in trips:
            c = lca(a, b)
            x = a
            while x != c:
                freq[x] += 1
                x = parent[x]
            freq[c] += 1
            y = b
            while y != c:
                freq[y] += 1
                y = parent[y]

        def dp(u, p):
            res0 = 0
            res1 = (price[u] - price[u] // 2) * freq[u]
            for v in g[u]:
                if v != p:
                    curr = dp(v, u)
                    res0 += max(curr)
                    res1 += curr[0]
            return [res0, res1]

        ans = 0
        for i in range(n):
            ans += freq[i] * price[i]
        return ans - max(dp(0, -1))
```

Let's double-check with example 1 using code:
- `dfs` root 0, parent[1]=0, parent[2]=1, parent[3]=1.
- trips:
  [0,3]: lca(0,3): level0=0, level3=2, d=2, b=parent[3]=1, d=1, b=parent[1]=0, d=0, a==b return0. x=0 while x!=0 none, freq[0]+=1. y=3 while y!=0: freq[3]+=1 y=1; freq[1]+=1 y=0. Good.
  [2,1]: lca(2,1): level2=2, level1=1, swap -> a=1,b=2, d=1, b=parent[2]=1, a==b return1. x=2 while x!=1: freq[2]+=1 x=1; freq[1]+=1. y=1 while y!=1 none. Good.
  [2,3]: lca(2,3): level2=2, level3=2, d=0, a!=b, a=parent[2]=1, b=parent[3]=1, return1. x=2 freq2+1 x=1; freq1+1; y=3 freq3+1 y=1. Good.
freq [1,3,2,2]. Good.

- node3 leaf: res0=0, res1=(6-3)*2=6.

ans=40, max=17, return23. Good.

```
from typing import List

class Solution:
    def minimumTotalPrice(self, n: int, edges: List[List[int]], price: List[int], trips: List[List[int]]) -> int:
        g = [[] for _ in range(n)]
        for u, v in edges:
            g[u].append(v)
            g[v].append(u)

        freq = [0] * n
        level = [0] * n
        parent = [0] * n

        def dfs(u, l, p):
            level[u] = l
            parent[u] = p
            for v in g[u]:
                if v != p:
                    dfs(v, l + 1, u)

        def lca(a, b):
            if level[a] > level[b]:
                a, b = b, a
            d = level[b] - level[a]
            while d:
                b = parent[b]
                d -= 1
            if a == b:
                return a
            while a != b:
                a = parent[a]
                b = parent[b]
            return a

        dfs(0, 0, -1)

        for a, b in trips:
            c = lca(a, b)
            x = a
            while x != c:
                freq[x] += 1
                x = parent[x]
            freq[c] += 1
            y = b
            while y != c:
                freq[y] += 1
                y = parent[y]

        def dp(u, p):
            res0 = 0
            res1 = (price[u] - price[u] // 2) * freq[u]
            for v in g[u]:
                if v != p:
                    curr = dp(v, u)
                    res0 += max(curr)
                    res1 += curr[0]
            return [res0, res1]

        ans = 0
        for i in range(n):
            ans += freq[i] * price[i]
        return ans - max(dp(0, -1))
```

```
from typing import List

class Solution:
    def minimumTotalPrice(self, n: int, edges: List[List[int]], price: List[int], trips: List[List[int]]) -> int:
        g = [[] for _ in range(n)]
        for u, v in edges:
            g[u].append(v)
            g[v].append(u)

        freq = [0] * n
        level = [0] * n
        parent = [0] * n

        def dfs(u, l, p):
            level[u] = l
            parent[u] = p
            for v in g[u]:
                if v != p:
                    dfs(v, l + 1, u)

        def lca(a, b):
            if level[a] > level[b]:
                a, b = b, a
            d = level[b] - level[a]
            while d:
                b = parent[b]
                d -= 1
            if a == b:
                return a
            while a != b:
                a = parent[a]
                b = parent[b]
            return a

        dfs(0, 0, -1)

        for a, b in trips:
            c = lca(a, b)
            x = a
            while x != c:
                freq[x] += 1
                x = parent[x]
            freq[c] += 1
            y = b
            while y != c:
                freq[y] += 1
                y = parent[y]

        def dp(u, p):
            res0 = 0
            res1 = (price[u] - price[u] // 2) * freq[u]
            for v in g[u]:
                if v != p:
                    curr = dp(v, u)
                    res0 += max(curr)
                    res1 += curr[0]
            return [res0, res1]

        ans = 0
        for i in range(n):
            ans += freq[i] * price[i]
        return ans - max(dp(0, -1))
```

=== FIXED CODE ===
from typing import List

class Solution:
    def minimumTotalPrice(self, n: int, edges: List[List[int]], price: List[int], trips: List[List[int]]) -> int:
        g = [[] for _ in range(n)]
        for u, v in edges:
            g[u].append(v)
            g[v].append(u)

        freq = [0] * n
        level = [0] * n
        parent = [0] * n

        def dfs(u, l, p):
            level[u] = l
            parent[u] = p
            for v in g[u]:
                if v != p:
                    dfs(v, l + 1, u)

        def lca(a, b):
            if level[a] > level[b]:
                a, b = b, a
            d = level[b] - level[a]
            while d:
                b = parent[b]
                d -= 1
            if a == b:
                return a
            while a != b:
                a = parent[a]
                b = parent[b]
            return a

        dfs(0, 0, -1)

        for a, b in trips:
            c = lca(a, b)
            x = a
            while x != c:
                freq[x] += 1
                x = parent[x]
            freq[c] += 1
            y = b
            while y != c:
                freq[y] += 1
                y = parent[y]

        def dp(u, p):
            res0 = 0
            res1 = (price[u] - price[u] // 2) * freq[u]
            for v in g[u]:
                if v != p:
                    curr = dp(v, u)
                    res0 += max(curr)
                    res1 += curr[0]
            return [res0, res1]

        ans = 0
        for i in range(n):
            ans += freq[i] * price[i]
        return ans - max(dp(0, -1))
